Method: 利用卫星物理运输带有稀土离子量子比特的纠缠处理器节点，在地面站之间分发纠缠；对信道损耗、误差和背景光进行了全面分析。

Result: 单颗卫星即可分发接近理论最大数量的纠缠对；EPR对保真度在短距离网络为99.3%，全球覆盖时为93.9%；白天在晴朗天空下可运行。

Conclusion: 基于运输的方案理论上优于其他卫星方案，可扩展至星座而不引入额外信道损耗，并可通过主动纠错或纠缠纯化进一步提高保真度。

Abstract: We propose a scheme for distributing entanglement over global distances in a heralded manner by using satellites to physically transport entangled processor nodes with rare-earth-ion qubits. A full analysis of channel losses, errors and background light is performed to determine the fidelity and number of entangled pairs that can be distributed between two ground stations. We show that the scheme works already with a single satellite and can distribute close to the theoretical maximum number of entangled pairs that can be generated in a satellite overpass. In addition, we argue that in theory transportation-based schemes outperform other satellite-based schemes and can be scaled up to a constellation without additional channel losses. Daytime operation seems feasible as long as the sky is clear, with an EPR pair fidelity ranging from 99.3% at shorter network lengths to 93.9% with global coverage and can be further improved by active error correction or entanglement purification.

</details>


### [91] [Certified Finite-Shot Operating Windows for Virtual Distillation and Symmetry Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15464)
*Vicenzo Scavino Alfaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限样本操作窗口理论，用于可证明地比较量子误差缓解方法（虚拟蒸馏VD与对称验证SV），推导了均方误差定律和选择三分法，通过理论预测（缩放指数、符号一致性）与实验验证（IBM量子设备、QAOA实例）表明不存在普适最优方法，而是存在由噪声率和样本预算决定的认证操作窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子误差缓解方法通常以无穷样本偏差进行比较，但在实际量子设备上，有限采样预算、估计器不稳定性和单次资源成本决定了方法的实际表现。现有理论缺乏对这些有限样本效应的可证明比较框架。

Method: 推导了虚拟蒸馏（VD）和对称验证（SV）的均方误差定律，包含显式非渐近余项常数；针对VD分析了商估计器的统计偏差和分母不稳定性并给出浓度认证；针对SV隔离了不可检测误差的偏差基底和接受概率导致的采样惩罚；提出选择三分法（平局、一致支配、真实权衡）和自一致性检验。

Result: 理论预测操作窗口位置按噪声率缩放为p^{-2}或p^{-1}，预注册实验验证了拟合指数-1.97（预测-2）和300/300符号一致性；在IBM量子设备上，理想VD窗口存在但实际干涉测量开销和分母不稳定性使其消失，校准SV在测试的QAOA实例中为实际胜者。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的量子误差缓解方法，而是存在由噪声率和样本预算决定的认证操作窗口结构，这一结论不是缓解方法的失败，而是认证操作窗口理论预测的机制结构。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation methods are usually compared through their infinite-shot bias, but on real devices the comparison is decided by finite sampling budgets, estimator instabilities, and per-shot resource costs. We develop a finite-shot operating-window theory that makes this comparison certifiable for virtual distillation (VD) and symmetry verification (SV): for each method we derive a mean-squared-error law with explicit, non-asymptotic remainder constants. For VD, the law captures the statistical bias and denominator instability of its quotient estimator, with a concentration certificate locating the sample size beyond which the quotient is trustworthy; for SV, it isolates the bias floor left by undetectable errors and the sampling penalty set by the acceptance probability. A selection trichotomy classifies any two-method comparison into a tie, uniform dominance, or a genuine tradeoff with a certified crossing window, including a self-consistency test that rejects spurious crossings. The theory makes falsifiable predictions -- operating-window locations scaling as $p^{-2}$ or $p^{-1}$ in the noise rate, and the sign pattern of all pairwise comparisons -- which exact white-box experiments confirm with fitted exponent $-1.97$ against the predicted $-2$ and with $300/300$ sign agreement, within a pre-registered analysis whose single failed gate, an over-strict all-instance criterion, is reported and audited in full. Gate-level simulation and archived runs on two IBM backends then test the windows under device conditions: idealized VD windows exist, but realistic interferometry overhead and denominator instability erase them, and calibrated SV is the practical winner in the tested QAOA instances. This absence of a universal winner is not a failure of mitigation; it is the regime structure that certified operating windows predict.

</details>


### [92] [Hardy and Cabello Arguments in Spatial and Temporal Frauchiger-Renner Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15467)
*Ehsan Erfani Maharat,Ali Ahanj,Mohsen Sarbishaei*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Frauchiger-Renner框架下Hardy和Cabello逻辑结构在空间和时间扩展中的表现，揭示空间纠缠与时间多观测者关联之间的结构差异，并验证观测者无关描述失败是否依赖于类空分离。

Method: 将Hardy和Cabello悖论嵌入FR多观测者架构，分别构建空间多观测者场景和基于单个自旋1/2系统的顺序时间FR协议，通过相干多观测者测量分析两种逻辑结构在不同场景下的违反情况。

Result: 空间场景：Cabello违反(Δ_max=0.1078)超过Hardy最大概率(0.09017)。时间场景：Hardy矛盾因顺序状态更新的动力学约束完全消失，Cabello型违反仍存但减弱(Δ_max≈0.0674)。

Conclusion: 空间纠缠和时间多观测者关联在FR型逻辑场景中存在根本结构差异；某些观测者无关描述失败不依赖于类空分离条件。

Abstract: We investigate Hardy- and Cabello-type logical structures within spatial and temporal extensions of the Frauchiger--Renner (FR) framework, embedding these constructions directly into the FR multi-observer architecture. In the spatial multi-observer scenario, both Hardy and Cabello contradictions arise, with the Cabello construction yielding the stronger violation,$\(Δ_{\rm Cabello}^{\max}=0.1078\)$, which exceeds the maximal Hardy probability $\(P_{H}^{\max}=\frac{5\sqrt{5}-11}{2}\approx 0.09017\)$. We then develop a sequential temporal FR protocol based on coherent multi-observer measurements performed on a single spin-$\tfrac12$ system. In this temporal setting, the Hardy contradiction disappears identically due to dynamical constraints imposed by sequential state updates, whereas a finite Cabello-type violation survives, \(Δ_{\rm Cabello}^{\max}\approx 0.0674\). Our results establish a fundamental structural distinction between spatial entanglement and temporal multi-observer correlations in FR-type logical scenarios, and demonstrate that certain observer-independent description failures persist even without spacelike separation.

</details>

*Zain H. Saleem*

TL;DR: 本文研究了双量子比特哈密顿量中相互作用参数产生纠缠的速度，推导了并发度导数与量子Fisher信息之间的上界关系 $|\partial_g C| \le \sqrt{F_Q^{(g)}}$，并给出了饱和条件，揭示了量子计量学与纠缠生成之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究纠缠生成速度与参数估计精度之间的内在联系，建立量子计量学中量子Fisher信息与纠缠资源生成速率的定量关系。

Method: 针对任意纯双量子比特态在一般非局域相互作用下的演化，推导并发度关于耦合参数的导数与量子Fisher信息之间的不等式关系，并分析饱和条件及达到最大速率的态与动力学区域。

Result: 得到 $|\partial_g C| \le \sqrt{F_Q^{(g)}}$ 的上界，表明纠缠生成速度受量子Fisher信息平方根的限制；确定了饱和条件，并发度在饱和时以量子态可区分性允许的最大速率演化。

Conclusion: 量子Fisher信息不仅决定了参数估计的精度极限，也同时限制了纠缠资源生成的最大速率，建立了量子计量学与纠缠动力学之间的基本联系。

Abstract: We investigate the speed at which entanglement can be generated by an interaction parameter encoded in a two-qubit Hamiltonian, quantified by the derivative of concurrence with respect to the coupling parameter. For arbitrary pure two-qubit states evolving under a general nonlocal interaction, we derive a bound relating this entanglement speed to the quantum Fisher information (QFI). Specifically, we show that $|\partial_g C| \le \sqrt{F_Q^{(g)}}$, where $F_Q^{(g)}$ is the QFI associated with estimation of the parameter. This establishes $\sqrt{F_Q}$ as a an upper bound on the speed of entanglement generation in parameter space. We further derive the saturation conditions and identify the states and dynamical regimes for which equality is attained. At saturation, concurrence evolves at the maximum rate permitted by the distinguishability of the underlying quantum state. These results reveal a direct connection between quantum metrology and entanglement generation, showing that the same information-theoretic quantity that governs parameter-estimation precision also limits the speed at which entanglement resources can be created.

</details>


### [94] [Orbital-optimized spin-adapted multistate contracted VQE for excited states and properties on quantum hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15489)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变分态方法中，随着电子态数量的增加，需要更表达的ansatz以平衡精度与电路复杂度，本文旨在解决这一根本性权衡问题。

Conclusion: oo-MC-VQE方法结合自旋适应算子能够有效计算基态和激发态，且在实际量子硬件上具有可行性。

Abstract: We introduce the orbital-optimized multistate contracted variational quantum eigensolver (oo-MC-VQE) method with spin-adapted operators for the computation of ground and excited states, as well as state-specific and transition properties. The use of spin-adapted operators ensures that the spin symmetry of the reference states is conserved throughout the VQE optimization. In multistate variational approaches, achieving a balanced description of an increasing number of electronic states places growing demands on the expressibility of the underlying ansatz, thereby introducing a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and circuit complexity. We consider the effects of this trade-off explicitly and find that the number of circuit parameters required to obtain accurate results is reported to scale approximately linearly in the number of states. We further present an explicit quantum-circuit implementation of the oo-MC-VQE method and demonstrate its integration with quantum error mitigation techniques. Finally, we execute the method on real quantum devices to compute absorption spectra for two benchmark molecular systems.

</details>


### [95] [Degeneracy Cannot Violate the Quantum Hamming Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15558)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Jing-Ling Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有精确二元量子子空间码（K>1）都必须满足量子Hamming界，即使存在退化性也无法突破该界限。证明通过将Li-Xing线性规划多项式转化为四元Hamming球的精确交集计数，并利用单调性和局部节点-边充电不等式完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近三十年来，退化性是否能规避量子Hamming界一直未得到完全解答。不同的可纠正错误可能对码空间产生相同作用，这使得传统的不交球论证失效，因此需要更一般的证明方法。

Method: 将Li-Xing线性规划多项式转化为四元Hamming球的精确交集计数；利用块长度和球心间隔的单调性将问题约化到最短可允许长度上的局部节点-边充电不等式。

Result: 证明了每个精确二元量子子空间码（K>1）都服从量子Hamming界，无需假定非退化性或可加性。退化性可以合并可纠正错误扇区，但不能扩大有限长二元Hamming界。

Conclusion: 退化性无法突破有限长量子Hamming界，该界限对所有精确二元量子子空间码都成立。

Abstract: The quantum Hamming bound is the standard finite-length sphere-packing bound for exact correction of arbitrary qubit errors. Whether degeneracy can evade this bound has remained unresolved in full generality for nearly three decades: distinct correctable errors may act identically on the code space, so the usual disjoint-sphere argument breaks down. We prove that every exact binary quantum subspace code with $K>1$ obeys the bound, without assuming either nondegeneracy or additivity. Our proof turns the Li--Xing linear-programming polynomial into an exact intersection count for quaternary Hamming balls. Monotonicity in block length and in ball-center separation then reduces the problem to a local node--edge charging inequality at the shortest admissible length. Thus degeneracy can merge correctable error sectors, but cannot enlarge the finite-length binary Hamming bound.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum Illumination with Symmetry-Constrained Random Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15586)
*Sooryansh Asthana,Sai Vinjanampathy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子照明中利用Haar随机探针态并约束在对称性分辨子空间中的性能，证明固定电荷扇区中的典型态能够达到与最大纠缠探针相同的渐近量子照明优势，揭示了对称性保护的相关子空间维度决定热噪声抑制和Chernoff指数，表明量子照明的操作资源可从精细调谐的探针态泛化为对称保护的大相关子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子照明中当探针态受对称性约束时，随机典型态能否达到与精心设计的最大纠缠态相同的量子优势，并揭示对称性在其中的作用机制。

Method: 利用量子信道辨别和渐近假设检验工具，推导了Haar随机探针系综的辨别指数，分析了对称性分辨子空间对探测性能的影响。

Result: 固定电荷扇区中的典型态能达到与最大纠缠探针相同的渐近量子照明优势；有效热噪声抑制和Chernoff指数由可访问对称性子空间的维度决定。

Conclusion: 量子照明的操作资源可从精细调谐的特定探针态结构泛化为存在大对称性保护的相关子空间，近优量子假设检验资源可自然地从受守恒律约束的通用多体量子态中涌现。

Abstract: Quantum illumination provides a quantum advantage in detecting weakly reflecting objects embedded in a noisy environment, even when environmental noise destroys most of the initial entanglement. We investigate this advantage using Haar-random probe states constrained to symmetry-resolved subspaces. Employing tools from quantum channel discrimination and asymptotic hypothesis testing, we derive the discrimination exponents associated with Haar-random probe ensembles and identify the role of symmetry in determining their performance. We show that typical states drawn from fixed-charge sectors achieve the same asymptotic quantum-illumination advantage as maximally entangled probes. In particular, we show that the effective thermal-noise suppression and the corresponding Chernoff exponent are governed by the dimension of the accessible symmetry sector. Our results reveal that the operational resource underlying quantum illumination can be generalized from fine-tuned structure of a specific probe state to the existence of a large symmetry-protected correlation subspace. These findings establish a direct connection between quantum illumination, symmetry-resolved typicality, and quantum channel discrimination, and demonstrate that near-optimal quantum hypothesis testing resources can emerge naturally from generic many-body quantum states constrained by conservation laws.

</details>


### [97] [A New Definition of Quantum Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15607)
*Stephen Bruce Sontz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出量子态叠加的通常描述存在歧义，因为希尔伯特空间中的求和运算不能直接下推到射影空间。作者在复射影空间的几何框架下，提出了一个严格的几何定义：两个纯态的叠加是它们所确定的唯一复直线。最后比较了叠加与正交基展开的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决量子态叠加概念在数学上长期存在的歧义性（Dirac早在1930年就已指出），为量子理论的几何相空间——复射影空间中的态叠加提供严格的几何定义。

Method: 在复射影空间的几何框架下，将两个纯态视为射影空间中的两个不同点，将它们的叠加定义为通过这两点的唯一复直线。

Result: 给出了一个无歧义的、几何化的态叠加定义，并与正交基展开进行了对比分析。

Conclusion: 在复射影空间中，两个纯态的叠加应被理解为它们所确定的唯一复直线，这为量子态叠加提供了严格的几何基础。

Abstract: The usual description of the superposition of two (pure quantum) states is ambiguous, since the binary operation of summation in a Hilbert space does not pass down to the quotient projective space. Even though Dirac noted this as early as 1930, it is often asserted that the superposition is a binary operation acting on two states with a value that is a unique state. The goal for this note is to motivate a rigorous, geometrical definition of the superposition of states in the setting of complex projective space, which has been argued elsewhere to be the natural geometric phase space for quantum theory. The upshot is that the new definition of the superposition of two pure states, viewed as two distinct points in the projective space, is the unique (complex) line on which those two points lie. Finally, a comparison is given between superposition and expansion in an orthonormal basis.

</details>


### [98] [Comparative Performance Analysis of NIST PQC Standards: From STM32 Software Limitations to FPGA-SoC Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15744)
*Mustafa Akif Yıldırım,Osman Tokluoglu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了NIST标准化后量子密码签名方案在资源受限嵌入式硬件上的实现挑战。对比分析表明，SPHINCS+在纯软件环境中几乎不可用，CRYSTALS-Dilithium参考实现也无法在MCU上完整执行。通过将专用NTT加速器集成到FPGA中实现硬件-软件协同设计，Dilithium在Zynq SoC上实现了毫秒级的密钥生成和签名生成性能，证明了纯软件PQC在标准微控制器上不可行，而硬件-软件协同方法可为抗量子嵌入式系统提供必要效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速进展对经典公钥密码系统构成严重威胁，亟需向后量子密码学过渡。然而，NIST标准化的PQC方案在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上的可行性尚未得到充分验证，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在ARM Cortex-M4 (STM32F407G)微控制器上对SPHINCS+和CRYSTALS-Dilithium进行纯软件实现对比分析；随后在Xilinx Zynq-7000 ZedBoard SoC上集成硬件加速的Dilithium核心，通过在FPGA中实现专用的数论变换(NTT)加速器来克服硬件限制。

Result: SPHINCS+在纯软件环境中执行时间不切实际，几乎不可用；Dilithium参考实现因RAM和时序约束无法在MCU上完整执行。而采用硬件-软件协同设计后，Dilithium在Zynq SoC上成功执行，密钥生成和签名生成性能达到毫秒级别。

Conclusion: 纯软件PQC方案在标准微控制器上不可行，但硬件-软件协同设计方法能够为抗量子嵌入式系统提供所需的效率，是实现量子安全嵌入式系统的可行路径。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of quantum computing poses a significant threat to classical public-key cryptographic systems, necessitating the transition to Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). This study investigates the implementation challenges of NISTstandardized signature schemes on resource-constrained embedded hardware. We present a comparative analysis of SPHINCS+ and CRYSTALS-Dilithium on an ARM Cortex-M4 (STM32F407G) microcontroller. Our findings reveal that SPHINCS+ is practically unusable in this software-only environment, with impractical execution times. Furthermore, the reference Dilithium implementation failed to execute entirely on the MCU due to severe RAM and timing constraints. To overcome these hardware limitations, we integrated a hardware-accelerated Dilithium core onto a Xilinx Zynq-7000 ZedBoard SoC. By implementing a specialized Number Theoretic Transform (NTT) accelerator in the FPGA fabric, we achieved successful execution with performance rates for key generation and signature generation at millisecond levels. These results demonstrate that while pure software PQC is non-viable for standard microcontrollers, a hardware-software codesign approach provides the necessary efficiency for quantumresistant embedded systems.

</details>


### [99] [Noise-Adaptive Predictive Dynamical Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15769)
*Ali Abu-Nada,Subhashish Banerjee,Lian-Ao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种噪声自适应动态去耦框架，结合开放量子系统建模与机器学习预测，针对随机电报噪声下的量子比特，通过实时预测并自适应施加控制脉冲，显著优于传统周期动态去耦方案，尤其在非马尔可夫和非平稳噪声环境中效果更突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际环境噪声对量子相干性的破坏是可扩展量子技术面临的根本障碍，传统动态去耦协议依赖固定脉冲时序，无法应对复杂时变噪声环境，亟需自适应的量子控制策略。

Method: 结合解析的开放量子系统建模与基于机器学习的短时相干演化预测，构建噪声自适应动态去耦框架；对随机电报噪声下的量子比特，根据预测的瞬时噪声动力学自适应施加控制脉冲。

Result: 数值仿真表明，机器学习辅助的自适应控制策略在使用相当数量控制脉冲的条件下，显著优于传统周期动态去耦方案，在非马尔可夫和非平稳噪声环境中性能提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 预测性机器学习辅助动态去耦是一种有前景且可扩展的自适应量子控制框架，适用于实际噪声量子器件。

Abstract: Protecting quantum coherence against realistic environmental noise remains one of the fundamental obstacles to scalable quantum technologies. We develop a noise-adaptive dynamical decoupling framework that combines analytical open-quantum-system modeling with machine-learning-based forecasting for a qubit interacting with random telegraph noise. Unlike conventional dynamical decoupling protocols based on fixed pulse schedules, the proposed approach continuously forecasts short-time coherence evolution and adaptively applies control pulses according to the instantaneous noise dynamics. We investigate stationary and non-stationary environments spanning both Markovian and non-Markovian regimes. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the machine-learning-assisted adaptive control strategy substantially outperforms conventional periodic dynamical decoupling while using a comparable number of control pulses. The improvement becomes particularly pronounced in non-Markovian and non-stationary regimes, where memory effects, coherence revivals, and temporally evolving noise strongly limit the effectiveness of static pulse protocols. These results establish predictive machine-learning-assisted dynamical decoupling as a promising and scalable framework for adaptive quantum control in realistic noisy quantum devices.

</details>


### [100] [MAPS: A Novel Multi-Axial Projective Sphere for Geometrically Visualizing Higher d-Valued Quantum State-Space of Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15801)
*Ali Al-Bayaty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了“多轴投影球(MAPS)”三维框架，用于可视化高维量子态（d≥3），解决了从二维量子比特到高维量子态可视化时面临的结构复杂性问题，并设计了新型d值相位轴门以操控量子态沿各轴旋转，该框架适用于机器学习、量子化学等高维数据可视化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特的二维量子态可通过布洛赫球优雅可视化，但扩展到更高维量子态（如qutrit、ququadit、quintit）时面临严重的结构与拓扑复杂性，亟需一种简化且自然的三维可视化框架。

Method: 提出“多轴投影球(MAPS)”框架，由n个投影相交空间轴构成（d-1 ≤ n ≤ 0），可沿这些轴旋转和移动d值量子态；同时提出了新型d值相位轴向门以操控量子态。

Result: MAPS框架在可视化简易性、结构简洁性和表征自然性方面优于现有方法，能有效可视化qutrit、ququadit、quintit等高维量子态，且每条轴可表示高维数据中的一个特征及其不同取值。

Conclusion: MAPS框架为高维量子态的可视化提供了通用解决方案，可拓展至机器学习、量子机器学习、量子化学等实际应用领域的数据可视化任务。

Abstract: Visualizing the d-valued quantum state-space of quantum systems serves as a foundational pillar for the scientific research and practical applications in quantum computing and information science, where d >= 2. The 2-valued quantum states of a qubit are elegantly visualized on the three-dimensional Bloch sphere. In contrast, expanding this geometrical paradigm to visualize higher d-valued quantum states of a qudit (d >= 3), e.g., a qutrit (d=3), ququadit (d=4), and quintit (d=5), leads to severe structural and topological complexities. This paper introduces a new generalized three-dimensional framework to effectively visualize higher d-valued quantum states of a qudit, in the aspects of ease of illustration, structural simplicity, and natural representation for researchers and engineers. We called this new framework the "multi-axial projective sphere (MAPS)", which consists of n projectional intersecting spatial axes, where d-1 <= n <= 0. We also propose a group of novel d-valued phase axial-based gates, to swivel and shift d-valued quantum states of a qudit along these n axes. Our generalized framework could be used for visualizing high-dimensional data for practical applications, e.g., machine learning, quantum machine learning, and quantum chemistry, where every axis of the MAPS represents a single feature of such data with its corresponding distinct values.

</details>


### [101] [Trainable Quantum Channels as Computational Primitives for Quantum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15808)
*Jingwei Wen,Ling Qian,Shijie Wei,Guilu Long*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可训练量子通道的非酉量子机器学习框架，通过开放系统动力学将量子通道重构为可训练计算基元，证明其输出为多个功能分量的结构化叠加，且有效可观测量谱可在训练中自适应调制，相较于酉变换的谱不变性有本质突破。该框架泛化了传统酉量子模型，并通过Kraus算子引入集成平均梯度和额外优化方向，在分类任务中验证了增强的优化动力学和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分量子学习受限于酉动力学，量子通道通常被视为有害噪声。本文旨在将量子通道重新定义为可训练计算资源，建立基于开放系统动力学的非酉量子机器学习框架，以突破酉变换的谱不变性限制并增强模型表达能力。

Method: 将量子通道形式化为可训练计算基元，构建非酉量子机器学习框架；通过分析有效可观测量谱的自适应调制机制揭示其与酉模型的本质区别；利用Kraus算子理论分析优化几何性质（集成平均梯度与额外优化方向）；在分类任务中使用可训练振幅阻尼和相位阻尼通道进行实证评估。

Result: 可训练量子通道的输出形成多个功能分量的结构化叠加；有效可观测量谱可在训练过程中自适应调制；框架泛化酉量子模型并引入额外非酉自由度；Kraus算子诱导的集成平均梯度和额外优化方向丰富了优化几何；可训练振幅阻尼和相位阻尼通道在分类任务中表现出增强的优化动力学和预测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种将量子通道作为可训练资源的原理性方法，推进了高性能量子学习架构的设计，证实了非酉动力学在量子机器学习中的核心价值。

Abstract: Variational quantum learning is traditionally constrained to unitary dynamics, often treating quantum channels as detrimental noise. In this work, we reformulate the quantum channels as trainable computational primitives and establish a non-unitary quantum machine learning framework grounded in open-system dynamics. We demonstrate that the outputs of channel-enhanced quantum models form a structured superposition of multiple functional components. Each component is governed by an effective observable whose spectrum can be adaptively modulated during training, a significant departure from the spectral invariance in unitary transformations. Moreover, the proposed framework generalizes conventional unitary quantum models by retaining them as a special case while introducing additional non-unitary degrees of freedom. Furthermore, we reveal that trainable quantum channels enrich the optimization geometry through ensemble-averaged gradient and additional optimization directions induced by the Kraus operators. Empirical evaluations on classification tasks using trainable amplitude-damping and phase-damping channels confirm enhanced optimization dynamics and predictive performance. Our work provides a principled approach for leveraging quantum channels as trainable resources and advances the design of high-performance quantum learning architectures.

</details>


### [102] [Complete Relational Description of Spin in a Quantum Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15873)
*Hannah Troger,Ofek Bengyat,Thomas D. Galley,Marios Christodoulou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在没有外部固定方向（经典背景）的情况下，仅通过与其他量子系统的关系来完全描述自旋状态的可能性。作者重新审视了Poulin提出的方法——通过对基础自旋和具有大角动量的参考自旋的联合状态进行旋转群平均，但该方法仅得到概率混合中的经典比特。作者证明，当量子参考系统增强为两个大自旋时，在参考系统量子数大的极限下，可以恢复自旋的标准量子力学描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋的量子力学标准描述预设了外部固定的方向（经典背景）。本文旨在探讨是否可以仅通过与其他量子系统的关系来完全描述自旋状态，而不依赖于经典背景。

Method: 作者重新审视了Poulin提出的群平均方法，但将量子参考系统从单个大角动量自旋增强为两个大自旋。通过分析在参考系统量子数大的极限下的行为，检验能否恢复自旋的标准量子力学描述。

Result: 当量子参考系统从单个大自旋增强为两个大自旋时，在参考系统量子数大的极限下，可以恢复自旋的标准量子力学描述，克服了Poulin方法仅能得到经典比特混合态的限制。

Conclusion: 通过使用两个大自旋作为量子参考系，可以在不依赖外部经典背景的情况下，在参考系统宏观极限下重建自旋的标准量子描述，这表明量子参考系的选择对量子描述的恢复至关重要。

Abstract: The standard description of the state of a spin in quantum mechanics presupposes externally fixed directions -- a classical background. Can a spin be fully described instead in relation to other quantum mechanical systems? Poulin suggested twenty years ago group averaging over rotations the joint state of a fundamental spin and a reference spin with large angular momentum which, however, yields a classical bit in a probabilistic mixture. We revisit this idea and show that when the quantum reference system is augmented to \emph{two} large spins, the standard quantum mechanical description of a spin is recovered in the limit of large quantum numbers for the reference system.

</details>


### [103] [A Gauge-Covariant Geometric Framework for Non-Hermitian Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15922)
*Gargi Das,Sudhanshu Shekhar,Bhabani Prasad Mandal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了准厄米非厄米量子系统的规范协变几何框架，以Dyson映射为核心几何对象，将其分解为拉伸和旋转分量，推导了非厄米贝里相位和量子几何张量，并揭示了例外点附近量子度量张量（~|ε_μ|⁻²）与贝里曲率（~|ε_μ|⁻³/²）的发散层级，最后在非厄米SSH和Hatano-Nelson模型中验证了标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具有实能谱和正定度规算符的准厄米非厄米量子系统建立一套完备的、规范协变的几何框架，以统一描述其中的度规形变、规范冗余和几何曲率等核心概念。

Method: 将Dyson映射提升为中心几何对象，构造Dyson联络并将其分解为厄米（旋转）和反厄米（拉伸）分量；利用规范协变态推导非厄米贝里相位和量子几何张量；分析例外点附近几何奇异性，并通过非厄米SSH和HN模型进行解析验证。

Result: 量子度量张量在例外点附近呈~|ε_μ|⁻²主导发散，贝里曲率呈~|ε_μ|⁻³/²次主导发散；非厄米几何曲率源于拉伸分量在算符层面的非对易性；在SSH和HN模型中精确验证了临界标度律。

Conclusion: 该几何框架系统性地统一了准厄米非厄米系统的几何结构，揭示了度畸形变驱动的非厄米几何本质，为理解例外点附近的几何奇异性提供了精确的定量描述。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive, gauge-covariant geometric framework for non-Hermitian quantum systems in the quasi-Hermitian regime, that is, the region of parameter space where the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian admits a real spectrum and a positive-definite metric operator. We build this framework by elevating the Dyson map to a central geometric object. This map is the transformation that converts a non-Hermitian Hamiltonian into an equivalent Hermitian one. From it we construct the Dyson connection and decompose it into Hermitian and anti-Hermitian parts, identified respectively as {\it stretching } and {\it rotation } components. This decomposition cleanly separates the genuine physical metric deformations from the unitary gauge redundancies. Working with manifestly gauge-covariant states, we then derive the complex non-Hermitian Berry phase and the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), and show that the non-Hermitian geometric curvature originates from the non-commutativity of the stretching components at the operator level. We further analyse the geometric singularities near an exceptional point (EP) and uncover a distinct hierarchy of divergences. For a general two-level non-Hermitian model, the quantum metric tensor (QMT) exhibits a leading-order divergence $\sim |ε_μ|^{-2}$, while the Berry curvature shows a weaker, subleading divergence $\sim |ε_μ|^{-3/2}$, with $ε_μ$ denoting the parameter displacement from the EP along an individual parameter axis $μ$. Finally, we examine physical realizations of this model, including the non-Hermitian Su--Schrieffer--Heeger (SSH) and Hatano--Nelson (HN) models, where exact analytical results confirm the predicted critical scaling laws and illustrate the metric-deformation-driven non-Hermitian geometries.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Measurement and Continuous Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15958)
*Chris Jackson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是2025年10月27日至12月11日在Perimeter Institute开设的关于扩散量子测量仪器的课程讲义，每周一和周四下午2:30至4:00授课，讲座录像可在指定网址获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供扩散量子测量仪器这一主题的系统性教学材料，帮助学习者理解相关理论和应用。

Method: 以课程讲义的形式呈现，包含系列讲座内容，并配有录像资源供参考。

Result: 形成了一套完整的扩散量子测量仪器课程讲义，并生成了可在线获取的讲座录像。

Conclusion: 该讲义为扩散量子测量仪器领域的学习提供了结构化教学资源，录像增强了可访问性。

Abstract: These are the lecture notes for a course on diffusive quantum measuring instruments. They were prepared and delivered at the Perimeter Institute on Mondays and Thursdays, from 2:30 to 4:00 PM, beginning October 27th, 2025 and ending December 11th, 2025. These lectures were recorded and can be found at \textbf{https://pirsa.org/c25038}.

</details>


### [105] [Learning ground state observables from quantum computing experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15983)
*Ben Jaderberg,Freya Shah,Minjun Jeon,M. Emre Sahin,Christa Zoufal,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在多达115个量子比特的二维海森堡XXZ模型中，利用近似基态产生的量子数据训练神经网络，成功预测了未见过哈密顿参数下的可观测物理量，实现了大规模二维相互作用量子系统从量子数据中学习的实际演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然理论上已建立了从量子数据学习基态性质的条件，但先前实验演示受限于小系统或高度结构化的态，主要原因是在量子处理器上制备多体基态存在困难。本文旨在突破这一局限，在更大规模和更实际的二维相互作用系统中验证这一范式。

Method: 在二维海森堡XXZ模型中制备近似基态（系统规模达115量子比特），构建包含单点位期望值、两点关联函数和12体环形关联函数的数据集，训练神经网络进行预测。

Result: 训练后的神经网络能够准确预测未见过的哈密顿参数下的空间分辨可观测物理量，包括训练分布内和接近相边界的外推分布区域。

Conclusion: 本文实际实现了在二维相互作用多体系统规模下从量子数据中学习，表明量子处理器提供的训练数据有望超越经典近似方法的范围，为未来的研究方向铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent theoretical progress has established conditions under which machine learning models can efficiently predict ground-state properties of gapped local Hamiltonians when trained on quantum-generated data. Previous experimental demonstrations in this paradigm, however, have largely been limited to small systems or highly structured states, due to the difficulty of preparing many-body ground states on quantum processors. In this work, we demonstrate learning from experimental quantum data generated from approximate ground states of the two-dimensional Heisenberg XXZ model with system sizes up to 115 qubits. We construct a dataset of single-site expectation values, two-point correlations, and 12-body loop correlations across the antiferromagnetic phase. We then train neural networks on this data and show that they can accurately predict spatially resolved observables for previously unseen Hamiltonian parameters, both within the training distribution and in an out-of-distribution regime approaching the phase boundary. Our results demonstrate the practical realization of learning from quantum data for an interacting two-dimensional many-body system at scale, motivating a path toward regimes where quantum processors could provide training data beyond the reach of classical approximation methods.

</details>


### [106] [Influence of the Electron's Anomalous Magnetic Dipole Moment on High-Atomic-Number Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15995)
*C. A. S. Almeida,J. Auto-Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，通过考虑电子的反常磁偶极矩（AMDM）贡献，即使在点核模型下，也能获得Z>137时正则的电子波函数和物理可接受的能级，无需依赖有限核尺寸效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强场QED中，Z>137时点核模型下电子波函数出现奇异性能量，通常需引入有限核尺寸来克服；本文试图探究电子反常磁矩是否足以独立解决该奇异性问题。

Method: 采用有效理论框架，在点核模型下将电子的反常磁偶极矩（AMDM）贡献纳入计算，分析其对狄拉克方程解的正则性和能级的影响。

Result: 发现仅通过计入AMDM贡献，即可获得Z>137时正则的波函数和物理上可接受的能级值，无需引入有限核尺寸。

Conclusion: 电子的反常磁偶极矩效应本身足以消除点核模型中Z>137的奇异性问题，为超重原子中强场QED现象（如自发正电子产生）提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: Super-heavy atoms ($Z > 100$) are usually studied in the context of the so-called ``Quantum Electrodynamics of Strong Fields''. In this theory the problem of the singularity in the electron energy whenever $Z > 137$ is overcome. This is done by considering the finite size of the nucleus and leads to interesting phenomena, such as the spontaneous production of positrons. Here, we show that taking into account the contribution from the Anomalous Magnetic Dipole Moment of the electron (by means of an effective theory), within a point-nucleus model, is a sufficient condition to obtain regular wave functions and physically acceptable energy values for $Z > 137$.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Algorithm for Open-System Battery Cathodes by Modeling Multiple Strongly Coupled Holstein Polarons with Chain-Mapped Caldeira-Leggett Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16017)
*Joshua M. Courtney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种容错量子算法，通过Caldeira-Leggett链映射将电池阴极锂化过程中的开放系统动力学（与电子、Li⁺和声子库交换）转化为可Trotter化的酉动力学，从而计算开路电压和微分容量等电化学可观测量。算法在LiFePO₄和LiMn₂O₄上验证了与实验的一致性，并给出了端到端容错资源估计：368个逻辑量子位，每步~3×10⁵个T门，完整电压曲线约1.7×10¹²个T门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池阴极锂化涉及强电子-晶格耦合的开放系统动力学，传统闭哈密顿量子模拟无法产生开路电压和微分容量等电化学可观测量，因为这些量由与电子、Li⁺和声子库的交换决定。需要开发能够处理开放系统动力学的容错量子算法。

Method: 通过酉链映射的Caldeira-Leggett嵌入将库浴Trotter化，将开放系统动力学转化为可量子模拟的酉演化；使用四阶Trotter步，T门计数与系统大小多项式相关；在强耦合下验证了与HEOM的一致性，在弱耦合下验证了Lindblad极限。

Result: 单载流子LiFePO₄的微分容量峰位于实验平台±5 mV可重复性内；多载流子LiMn₂O₄的双平台电压曲线分裂125 mV，与实验150 mV相差17%；资源估计：368逻辑量子位，每步~3×10⁵ T门，完整电压曲线~1.7×10¹² T门（可并行化至~10³条轨迹）。

Conclusion: 该算法不仅适用于电池化学，还可复现宏观量子相干、两带超导和MSW共振，表明动态电池化学及类似哈密顿量在容错量子模拟的适用范围之内，生产规模的动态运行是未来硬件的里程碑。

Abstract: Cathode lithiation occupies a chemical regime of tightly localized orbitals, narrow bandwidths, and strong electron-lattice coupling. The defining electrochemical observables (open-circuit voltage and differential capacity) are open-system, reservoir-equilibration quantities that closed-Hamiltonian quantum simulation cannot produce, set by exchange with electron, Li$^+$, and phonon baths. We present a fault-tolerant quantum algorithm that recovers them through a unitary chain-mapped Caldeira-Leggett embedding, rendering the baths Trotterizable. The resulting fourth-order Trotter step has a T-gate count polynomial in system size, validating its open-system dynamics against hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) at strong coupling and the Lindblad limit at weak coupling. For single-carrier olivine LiFePO$_4$, a single voltage anchor on an otherwise DFT-fixed Hamiltonian places the differential-capacity peak within the $\pm5$ mV reproducibility of the experimental plateau. For multi-carrier spinel LiMn$_2$O$_4$, whose $1{:}1$ Mn$^{3+}$/Mn$^{4+}$ filling makes the inter-site Coulomb repulsion dynamically active, the same kernel yields a two-plateau voltage curve with a $125$ mV split, within $17\%$ of the observed $150$ mV. We deliver an end-to-end fault-tolerant resource estimate for such a multi-carrier, three-reservoir observable: $368$ logical qubits and $\sim3\times10^5$ T-gates per step, or $\sim1.7\times10^{12}$ T-gates for a full voltage curve (parallelizable over $\sim10^3$ trajectories), leaving the production-scale dynamical run as a milestone for future hardware. The same kernel reproduces macroscopic quantum coherence, two-band superconductivity, and the Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein resonance without modification, placing dynamical battery chemistry and similar Hamiltonians within scope for fault-tolerant quantum simulation.

</details>


### [108] [Adiabatically-induced Kawaguchi geometry and jerk in quantum-classical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16037)
*Athanasios Chatzistavrakidis,Larisa Jonke,Ryan Requist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过近单位幺正变换序列，从混合量子-经典系统中绝热消除量子自由度，推导出经典变量的有效作用量层级，发现三阶欧拉-拉格朗日方程为非牛顿型（依赖加加速度），并在经典变量空间诱导出Kawaguchi几何（具有近辛结构和依赖于加速度的线元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合量子-经典系统中，绝热消除量子自由度会产生经典运动方程中的有效力。现有方法可对绝热参数进行任意阶展开以生成高阶力，但缺乏系统化的有效作用量构建框架。

Method: 对量子态施加一系列近单位幺正变换，系统性地推导出经典变量的有效作用量层级，并在三阶得到非牛顿型运动方程，进而从几何角度分析该系统的结构。

Result: 三阶欧拉-拉格朗日方程中力依赖于位置的三阶时间导数（加加速度）；在经典变量空间上诱导出特殊的Kawaguchi几何，该几何具有近辛结构，且线元同时依赖于速度和加速度。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效捕捉分子动力学模拟中的高阶非绝热效应。

Abstract: Adiabatically eliminating the quantum degrees of freedom in a mixed quantum-classical system produces an effective force in the classical equation of motion. The elimination can be made to any order in the adiabatic parameter, generating a series of higher order forces. By applying a sequence of near-identity unitary transformations to the quantum state, we derive a hierarchy of increasingly accurate effective actions for the classical variables. The third order Euler-Lagrange equation is non-Newtonian as the force depends on the jerk, the third order time derivative of position. We find that the third order terms induce a special kind of Kawaguchi geometry on the space of classical variables. This geometry is characterized by an almost symplectic structure and a differential line element that depends on the acceleration in addition to the velocity. Our results can be used to efficiently capture higher order nonadiabatic effects in molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [109] [Readout-Induced Leakage in Superconducting Circuits with Nonlinear Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16055)
*Sumeru Hazra,Wei Dai,Daniel K. Weiss,Pranav D. Parakh,Luigi Frunzio,Michel H. Devoret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究了超导电路中本征非线性耦合对读取诱导跃迁的影响，发现这种耦合并不能自动抑制有害跃迁，反而可能引入额外寄生过程，需通过合理设计才能发挥优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动诱导的不必要跃迁限制了超导电路的读取速度和保真度，导致量子纠错中的关联错误。本征非线性耦合被认为是替代传统线性混合的有前景方案，但其实际效果需要严格检验。

Method: 作者通过理论分析，比较了本征非线性耦合与传统线性耦合下驱动诱导跃迁的行为，研究了读取频率变化对跃迁速率的影响，并分析了寄生过程的引入机制。

Result: 本征非线性耦合本身不能消除甚至不一定能抑制驱动诱导跃迁；未经适当设计时反而引入额外寄生过程；读取频率的微小变化（~7%）可导致泄漏率变化数个数量级。

Conclusion: 本征非线性耦合的优势只有在通过合理器件设计（包括辅助模式的频谱布局和寄生模式的消除）时才能实现，而非天然具备优越性。

Abstract: In superconducting circuits, drive-induced unwanted transitions limit the readout power, thereby constraining readout speed and fidelity. When such transitions excite the qubit into leakage states, they produce correlated errors that are particularly harmful for quantum error correction. Native nonlinear qubit-readout resonator coupling is a promising alternative to conventional linear hybridization because it provides intrinsic Purcell protection and stricter selection rules for multiphoton processes. In realistic devices, however, we show that such a coupling alone neither eliminates nor necessarily suppresses drive-induced transitions. Instead, if not appropriately engineered, these couplings often worsen the situation by introducing additional parasitic processes. Moreover, the rates of these unwanted transitions remain sensitive to the choice of readout frequency, regardless of the coupling mechanism. We demonstrate that readout-induced leakage can thus vary by orders of magnitude even when readout frequencies differ by less than ~7%. Our results establish that the benefits of native nonlinear couplings are realized only through informed device design, including the spectral placement of relevant auxiliary modes and elimination of parasitic ones.

</details>


### [110] [Enhanced Sensitivity near a Quantum Exceptional Point in the Absence of Engineered Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16060)
*Réouven Assouly,Harry Hanlim Kang,Aziza Almanakly,Michael A. Gingras,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Hannah Stickler,Mollie E. Schwartz,Kyle Serniak,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导电路中实现了一个封闭四模量子系统，通过参量耦合模拟PT对称二聚体动力学，并在非量子极限区域观测到奇异点附近的增强传感灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非厄米系统实现依赖增益和损耗，严重限制了量子相干性；本文旨在通过封闭量子系统模拟PT对称动力学，避免工程化耗散对量子相干性的破坏。

Method: 利用超导共面波导谐振器的四个谐波模式作为子系统，通过电流泵浦SNAIL器件实现参量耦合，构建封闭四模系统模拟PT二聚体（两个耦合谐振器具有平衡增益和损耗）的动力学行为。

Result: 通过信噪比测量，在非量子极限区域观察到奇异点附近对PT二聚体耦合强度微小变化的增强灵敏度。

Conclusion: 封闭量子系统可以模拟非厄米PT对称动力学并实现奇异点增强传感，且避免了传统增益-损耗方案对量子相干性的限制。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian systems exhibit phenomena absent from Hermitian systems, including exceptional points (EPs), at which two or more eigenvectors coalesce. Conventional implementations rely on gain and loss, which strongly limit quantum coherence. Here, following a proposal by Wang and Clerk (PRA 2019), we realize a closed four-mode quantum system that emulates the dynamics of a PT dimer - two coupled resonators with balanced gain and loss - without engineered dissipation. The four modes are implemented as harmonics of a superconducting coplanar-waveguide resonator, with parametric couplings engineered using a current-pumped SNAIL. We use this device as a sensor for small variations in the PT dimer coupling strength. From signal-to-noise-ratio measurements, we observe enhanced sensitivity near the EP in a non-quantum-limited regime.

</details>


### [111] [Enhancing Quantum Machine Learning with Anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16090)
*Da Zhang,Wen-Qiang Liu,Zhaohui Wei,Zhang-Qi Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一玻色子、费米子和任意子（分数）交换统计的量子核框架，从表示能力、核几何和学习基准三个角度系统比较了不同交换统计对量子机器学习性能的影响。研究发现任意子核一致优于玻色子和费米子核，表明粒子交换统计是量子机器学习中一个被忽视但重要的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算和量子机器学习的核心在于利用量子现象作为计算资源，但粒子交换统计（交换相位）在这一领域的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 作者建立了一个统一不同交换统计（玻色子、费米子、任意子）的量子核框架，并从三个层面进行分析：(1) 表示层面：通过Haar平均有效维度分析；(2) 核几何层面：分析Gram矩阵与可区分粒子基线的分离程度及标签依赖模型复杂度；(3) 学习基准层面：评估目标对齐和类别几何特性。

Result: 任意子核在三个层面均表现最优：有效维度分析显示分数交换相位可访问对称/反对称极限无法触及的特征空间方向；Gram矩阵与可区分粒子基线分离更大且模型复杂度更低；学习基准上目标对齐更强、类别几何更有利。

Conclusion: 交换统计重塑了量子特征空间的结构和几何，能显著提升学习性能。粒子交换统计是量子机器学习中一个被忽视的计算资源，本文首次对不同交换相位下的量子学习模型进行了系统比较。

Abstract: The power of quantum computing and quantum machine learning relies on harnessing uniquely quantum phenomena as computational resources. While superposition, coherence and entanglement have been central to this effort, the role of particle exchange statistics remains largely unexplored. Here, we introduce a quantum kernel framework that unifies bosonic, fermionic, and anyonic (fractional) exchange statistics within a single learning paradigm. We study this family of kernels from three perspectives. At the representation level, Haar-averaged effective-dimension analysis shows that fractional exchange phases access feature-space directions inaccessible to the purely symmetric or antisymmetric limits. At the level of kernel geometry, the corresponding Gram matrices show greater separation from the distinguishable-particle baseline and reduced label-dependent model complexity. Finally, on learning benchmarks, anyonic kernels consistently outperform their bosonic and fermionic counterparts, with stronger target alignment and more favorable class geometry. Together, these findings show that exchange statistics reshape the structure and geometry of quantum feature space, leading to enhanced learning performance. Our work identifies particle exchange statistics as an overlooked computational ingredient for quantum machine learning and provides the first systematic comparison of quantum learning models across exchange phases.

</details>


### [112] [The Distribution Postulate in Algorithmic Bohmian Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16165)
*Jeffrey A. Barrett,Eddy Keming Chen,Josiah Lopez-Wild*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用算法随机性理论，将玻姆力学中的分布公设表述为一种客观约束法则，提出了算法玻姆力学（aBM），保证标准玻恩统计在极限下成立，而非仅具有高概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻姆力学需要分布公设来做出正确的经验预测，但该条件的理解方式不清晰，需要更精确的表述。

Method: 利用算法随机性理论将分布公设形式化为客观约束法则，对允许的量子力学状态和测量做出规定，构建算法玻姆力学（aBM）。

Result: aBM保证了一组典型量子实验在极限下标准玻恩统计的成立，而非仅以高概率成立；提供了尖锐的典型性条件。

Conclusion: 算法分布公设澄清了确定论量子理论中概率的地位，并展示了算法随机性理论如何帮助规定物理定律的内容。

Abstract: In order to make the right empirical predictions Bohmian mechanics requires a special statistical boundary condition -- the distribution postulate -- but it is unclear how best to understand this condition. We show how one might use the theory of algorithmic randomness to formulate the distribution postulate as an objective constraining law. The framework requires us to say something about admissible quantum-mechanical states and measurements. In return, algorithmic Bohmian mechanics (aBM) guarantees the standard Born statistics for a collection of canonical quantum experiments in the limit, not just with high probability. The algorithmic distribution postulate provides a sharp typicality condition, clarifies the status of quantum probabilities in the deterministic theory, and provides a concrete example of how notions provided by the theory of algorithmic randomness can aid in specifying the content of a physical law.

</details>


### [113] [Efficient Magic State Factory Via Transversal Non-Clifford Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16199)
*I-Chi Chen,Hrushikesh Pramod Patil,Huiyang Zhou,Andrew Sornborger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对基于码切换的魔法态制备与魔法态培育两种容错量子计算方法进行了端到端仿真比较，开发了双色码与旋转表面码之间的晶格手术转移协议，将码切换协议扩展到d=5双色码，并提出了两种结合相位反冲检查与横向非Clifford门的容错魔法态制备协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管码切换魔法态制备和魔法态培育在理论和实验上都取得了成功，但两者之间缺乏全面的比较分析，需要明确各自的资源需求和性能优劣。

Method: 采用端到端仿真方法，开发了双色码与旋转表面码之间的晶格手术转移协议，将码切换协议扩展到d=5双色码，并提出了结合相位反冲检查与横向非Clifford门的新协议。

Result: 完成了码切换魔法态制备的端到端仿真，实现了到d=5双色码的扩展，并提出了两种新型容错魔法态制备协议。

Conclusion: 系统比较了两种魔法态制备方法，为容错量子计算中的魔法态制备提供了新的技术路线和优化方向。

Abstract: Magic-state preparation is a central component of fault-tolerant quantum computing. Recent theoretical and experimental successes in code-switch-based magic-state preparation have underscored the promise of these methods for quantum error correction. Similarly, magic-state cultivation has likewise been demonstrated in both numerical and experimental settings. However, a thorough comparison between magic-state cultivation and code-switch-based magic-state factories is still missing. In this work, we carry out end-to-end simulations of magic-state preparation using code switching and compare its resource requirements and performance against magic-state cultivation. As part of this analysis, we develop a lattice-surgery protocol for transfer between the doubled color code and the rotated surface code. We extend the complete code-switching protocol to the $d=5$ doubled color code and perform the corresponding end-to-end simulations. Finally, we propose two fault-tolerant magic-state preparation protocols that combine phase-kickback checks with a transversal non-Clifford gate.

</details>


### [114] [QALM: Escaping Local Minima via Interleaved Exploration and Exploitation in Quantum Circuit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16221)
*Aidan Wagner,Mingkuan Xu,Pengyu Liu,Zhihao Jia,Umut A. Acar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出混合框架QALM，结合搜索算法的穷举探索能力与基于规则的优化效率，在保持规则系统计算效率的同时，实现了优于纯规则和纯搜索方法的电路缩减率，在248个电路测试中，同等时间预算下83.9%的电路达到或超过最强基线保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子电路优化器面临根本性限制：贪心规则优化器高效但易陷入局部最小值，搜索优化器能逃离局部最小值但需要指数级步数盲目探索高成本区域，无法在合理时间内判断邻域是否包含更深局部最小值。

Method: 提出QALM混合框架，交错执行搜索算法的穷举探索与基于规则的优化，利用两者的互补优势实现逃离局部最小值而不牺牲运行时效率。

Result: 在248个电路的综合评估中，QALM在相同时间预算下，对83.9%的电路达到或超过最强基线的保真度；电路缩减率优于现有基于规则和基于搜索的优化器，同时保持规则系统的计算效率。

Conclusion: 混合框架能够克服纯搜索和纯规则方法的固有局限，实现超越两者的性能，证明搜索与规则优化并非只能二选一，而是可以有机结合以取得更优效果。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimizers face a fundamental limitation in how they tolerate temporary cost increases. At one extreme, greedy rule-based optimizers immediately apply any cost-reducing transformation, achieving high efficiency but quickly becoming trapped in local minima. At the other extreme, search-based optimizers accept cost-increasing moves to explore the circuit space and escape such minima. However, because search-based optimizers cannot determine within a reasonable time budget whether a given point is promising, that is, whether its neighborhood contains a deeper local minimum, they must blindly explore higher-cost regions. As a result, escaping the current basin to reach a promising point takes exponentially many steps.
  In this work, we show that this limitation can be overcome with a hybrid framework that interleaves the exhaustive exploration capabilities of search algorithms with the efficiency of rule-based optimization. We implement this framework as QALM, a novel optimizer designed to escape local minima without incurring the runtime penalties of pure search. Crucially, our results demonstrate that QALM does not merely strike a balance; it outperforms existing rule-based and search-based optimizers in circuit reduction rates while operating with the computational efficiency of rule-based systems. In a comprehensive evaluation across 248 circuits, QALM matches or exceeds the fidelity of the strongest baseline on 83.9% of these circuits, given the same time budget.

</details>


### [115] [Bi-qutrit entangled edge states of positive partial transposes with largest ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16265)
*Kyung Hoon Han,Seung-Hyeok Kye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于双qutrit量子系统中任意一个由Schmidt秩为3的向量张成的正交补的8维子空间E，都存在以E为值域、部分转置秩为6的PPT纠缠边缘态，这是可能的最大秩。由此构造了一大类(8,6)型双qutrit PPT纠缠边缘态族，它们构成所有PPT态凸集的某个面，并在这些面的边界上发现了其他类型的双qutrit PPT纠缠边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双qutrit系统中具有最大可能部分转置秩的PPT纠缠边缘态的存在性和构造，以深化对PPT态凸集边界结构（尤其是其面结构）的理解。

Method: 利用Schmidt秩为3的向量张成的正交补来刻画8维子空间E，构造以E为值域的部分转置秩为6的PPT纠缠边缘态，并分析这些态在PPT态凸集的面结构中的位置。

Result: 成功构造了一大类(8,6)型双qutrit PPT纠缠边缘态族，并发现这些态构成PPT态凸集的面，同时在这些面的边界上找到了其他类型的双qutrit PPT纠缠边缘态。

Conclusion: (8,6)型是双qutrit系统中可能的最大部分转置秩的PPT纠缠边缘态类型，该构造揭示了一种丰富的族结构，有助于理解PPT态凸集的几何特性。

Abstract: Whenever $E$ is an eight dimensional subspace of the bi-qutrit quantum system whose orthogonal complement is spanned by a vector of Schmidt rank three, we show that there exist PPT entangled edge states with the range space $E$ whose partial transposes are of rank six, which is the largest possible rank. In this way, we exhibit a huge family of bi-qutrit PPT entangled edge states of type $(8,6)$. They make faces of the convex set of all PPT states, and we find bi-qutrit PPT entangled edge states of other types on the boundaries of such faces.

</details>


### [116] [Optimizing resource bounds in direct fidelity estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16336)
*Netanel Barel,Lee Peleg,Yotam Kadish,Amit Ben Kish,Yotam Shapira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在直接保真度估计中，利用噪声的结构化信息（如典型信道）可以显著降低所需采样设置数量，同时通过重新分配两个概率步骤的误差预算可进一步优化测量成本。数值实验展示了结构化噪声假设下的改进效果以及原始最坏情况界的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接保真度估计的现有最坏情况置信界忽略了实验噪声的结构化特征，导致资源边界过于保守。作者旨在利用噪声的额外结构信息来收紧资源上界，降低测量成本。

Method: 1) 引入结构化噪声假设（典型信道），分析其对有效Pauli采样设置数量的影响；2) 将总精度和置信度预算在两个概率步骤（随机采样Pauli可观测量、有限测量次数估计期望值）之间重新分配，转化为约束优化问题求解；3) 采用Qiskit进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 1) 在典型信道下，有效Pauli采样设置数量可大幅减少；2) 重新分配误差预算后，一般纯态场景的平均测量次数降低；3) 数值实验证实结构化噪声假设能显著改进性能，原始最坏情况界确实过于保守。

Conclusion: 直接保真度估计应考虑实际噪声结构，否则最坏情况界会过度保守；通过优化误差分配和利用噪声结构，可显著降低实验测量成本。

Abstract: Direct fidelity estimation provides a way to estimate the fidelity between an experimentally prepared state and a desired pure target state without performing full tomography. Two influential formulations were introduced in 2011 by Flammia and Liu and by da Silva, Landon-Cardinal, and Poulin. In these protocols, the total estimation error is controlled through two distinct probabilistic steps: first, the fidelity is approximated using randomly sampled Pauli observables; second, each sampled expectation value is estimated from finitely many measurement outcomes. In this work we show that additional structural information about the noise can substantially sharpen the corresponding resource bounds. In particular, for some canonical channels the effective number of sampled Pauli settings can be reduced, leading to lower measurement cost both in the general pure-state setting and in the case of a stabilizer state. These results illustrate a broader point: worst-case confidence bounds in direct fidelity estimation can be significantly conservative when experimentally relevant structure is ignored. As a technical ingredient, we also revisit the allocation of the total accuracy and confidence budgets between the two probabilistic steps. Reformulating the analysis in terms of separate error parameters yields a constrained optimization problem whose solution lowers the average number of measurements in the general pure-state setting. Numerical simulations based on quantum circuits implemented in Qiskit illustrate both the improvement obtained under structured-noise assumptions and the conservativeness of the original worst-case bounds.

</details>


### [117] [Scalable Graph State Generation with O(1) Local Feedforward in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16375)
*Xiaoyi Zheng,Chan-Tong Lam,Lin Chen,Zheng Xing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于本地测量和经典前馈的量子网络路由协议，将决策复杂度降低至均摊O(1)，确保延迟远小于量子比特相干时间，并在双物种离子阱平台上验证了其资源效率和时间可行性。通过擦除变换、空间复用和分支独立性抑制噪声，能够生成高保真度星型子图，作为通用模块合并构建任意子图，为未来容错分布式量子计算提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络面临概率性远程纠缠生成与有限相干时间之间的矛盾。现有路由协议依赖全局状态计算或路径优化，随着网络规模扩大，经典延迟累积加剧退相干，导致纠缠保真度下降。因此需要将路由决策延迟降至远低于量子比特相干时间的水平。

Method: 提出基于本地测量和经典前馈的路由协议，将本地决策复杂度降至均摊O(1)。将该协议映射到双物种 trapped-ion 平台进行混合模拟，结合擦除变换、空间复用和分支独立性来抑制噪声。

Result: 该协议在资源效率和时间可行性方面表现良好。读出保真度是主要瓶颈，但通过擦除变换结合空间复用和分支独立性可有效抑制噪声，确保高保真度星型子图的生成。

Conclusion: 该协议为生成高保真度星型子图提供了清晰路径，这些子图可作为通用模块合并构建任意子图，为未来容错分布式量子计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The development of quantum networks faces a key challenge: the contradiction between probabilistic long-range entanglement generation and finite coherence time. Existing routing protocols typically focus on global state computation or path optimization. As the network scales up, classical delays accumulate and exacerbate decoherence, leading to a decrease in entanglement fidelity. To reduce routing decision delays to levels far below the coherence time of qubits, we propose a protocol based on local measurement and classical feedforward. This protocol reduces the local decision complexity to amortized O(1) level, ensuring that the decision delay is always much smaller than the coherence time of qubits. We map this protocol onto a dual-species trapped-ion platform and perform hybrid simulations. The results show that the proposed protocol performs well in terms of both resource efficiency and time feasibility. Noise analysis indicates that readout fidelity is the main bottleneck of this protocol, but noise suppression can be achieved by employing an erasure transformation in the dual-species architecture, combined with spatial multiplexing and branch independence, thereby ensuring the generation of high-fidelity star subgraphs. This protocol provides a clear path to achieving high-fidelity star subgraphs. These subgraphs can serve as general modules, merging to construct arbitrary subgraphs, providing a feasible solution for future fault-tolerant distributed quantum computing.

</details>


### [118] [A short proof of the modified Kretschmann-Schlingemann-Werner conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16418)
*Satvik Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于任意两个量子信道Φ₁, Φ₂，存在一个酉算子U使得其Stinespring等距算子V₁与(𝟙⊗U)V₂之间的无穷范数距离不超过√(2‖Φ₁-Φ₂‖⋄)，从而肯定地解决了vom Ende对Kretschmann-Schlingemann-Werner猜想的修正版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kretschmann-Schlingemann-Werner猜想旨在建立量子信道之间距离与其Stinespring表示中等距算子之间距离的定量关系，是量子信息理论中关于信道表示稳定性的基本问题。vom Ende对此提出了修正版本，本文的目标是证明该修正猜想成立。

Method: 通过严谨的算子理论分析，利用量子信道的Stinespring dilation表示，在公共扩张空间上构造酉算子U，并建立信道完全有界范数距离（金刚范数）与等距算子无穷范数距离之间的不等式关系。

Result: 得到了上界不等式‖V₁-(𝟙⊗U)V₂‖∞ ≤ √(2‖Φ₁-Φ₂‖⋄)，表明信道距离的平方根控制着等距算子距离的上界。

Conclusion: 成功解决了vom Ende对Kretschmann-Schlingemann-Werner猜想的修正版本，为量子信道的表示稳定性提供了更精确的定量刻画。

Abstract: Let $Φ_1, Φ_2 : \mathbb{M}_d(\mathbb{C})\to \mathbb{M}_n(\mathbb{C})$ be two quantum channels with respective Stinespring isometries $V_1, V_2 : \mathbb{C}^{d}\to \mathbb{C}^{n} \otimes \mathbb{C}^{m}$ on any common dilation space $\mathbb{C}^{m}$. We prove that there exists a unitary $U$ on $\mathbb{C}^{m}$ such that $\|V_1-({\bf1}\otimes U)V_2\|_\infty\leq\sqrt{2\|Φ_1-Φ_2\|_\diamond},$ thus resolving vom Ende's modification of the Kretschmann-Schlingemann-Werner conjecture in the affirmative.

</details>


### [119] [Worst-case depth hierarchy for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16425)
*Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Michael de Oliveira,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian,Xingjian Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于QNC⁰的深度层次定理：对于每个d≥12，存在一类两轮交互问题，深度为d-1的量子电路无法达到近乎完美的成功率，而深度稍大的量子电路可以解决；同时，所有次对数深度的经典有界扇入电路都失败，展示了QNC⁰相对于NC⁰的无条件量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界深度经典电路已有成熟的层次定理，但常数深度量子计算（QNC⁰）的内部结构尚未被充分探索。主要障碍在于量子电路的下界技术稀缺。

Method: 利用约束系统与非局域博弈之间的对应关系，将群论构造转化为刚性算子值约束系统，再转化为非局域博弈。通过分析深度如何精细地影响电路在其输出量子比特间实现非局域关联的能力，将问题归结为非局域酉合成问题。

Result: 对于每个d≥12，构造了一族两轮交互问题，使得：(1) 深度d-1的量子电路（无论门集、电路大小或辅助量子比特如何）无法达到近乎完美的成功；(2) 深度大于d的简单有界扇入量子电路可实现；(3) 所有次对数深度的经典有界扇入电路均失败。

Conclusion: 增加深度会在QNC⁰内严格增加计算能力，建立了一个真正的量子层次结构。

Abstract: Circuit depth is a central resource in complexity theory. While bounded-depth classical circuits admit well-understood hierarchy theorems, the internal structure of constant-depth quantum computation remains comparatively unexplored.
  We prove an explicit depth hierarchy theorem for $\mathsf{QNC}^0$. For each $d\ge 12$, we construct a family of two-round interactive problems on which no depth-$(d-1)$ quantum circuit can achieve near-perfect success, regardless of gate set, circuit size, or ancillary qubits. In contrast, we prove that our construction admits realizations by simple bounded fan-in quantum circuits of depth larger than $d$ by a small constant factor. Moreover, all bounded fan-in classical circuits of sublogarithmic depth (in the input size) fail to achieve perfect success on these tasks for every $d$, yielding a hierarchy of problems that show unconditional quantum advantage of $\mathsf{QNC}^0$ over $\mathsf{NC}^0$.
  A key obstacle is the scarcity of lower bound techniques for quantum circuits. To address this, we develop methods to analyze how depth affects a circuit's ability to realize nonlocal correlations amongst its output qubits in a fine-grained manner. Our approach exploits the correspondence between constraint systems and nonlocal games, translating group-theoretic constructions into rigid operator-valued constraint systems and then into non-local games. In particular, we construct constraint systems whose unique faithful operator-valued solutions require every perfect strategy, and every near-perfect strategy to a fixed precision, to implement multi-controlled phase operations. This reduces to a nonlocal unitary-synthesis problem, yielding depth lower bounds for both shallow quantum and classical circuits.
  These results show that increasing depth strictly increases computational power within $\mathsf{QNC}^0$, establishing a genuinely quantum hierarchy.

</details>


### [120] [Detecting basis-dependent hardware errors through spatio-temporal quantum steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16451)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Kuo-Feng Chiu,Yi-Te Huang,Jhen-Dong Lin,Tse-Ming Chen,Yueh-Nan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对时空量子导引中无信令时间（NSIT）条件因有限测量统计量可能产生假阳性违反的问题，构建了统计假设检验框架，利用切比雪夫不等式给出无分布假设的p值上界。在超导处理器单量子比特态转移实验中识别出基依赖硬件错误，并在IonQ七量子比特Hayden-Preskill遥传协议中即使平均保真度超过经典阈值时也拒绝零假设，表明非经典性度量消失。强调统计假设检验在近端量子器件基依赖错误检测中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空量子导引中的NSIT条件违反可指示基依赖硬件错误，但有限测量统计量会导致假阳性违反，混淆真实硬件错误的检测，因此需要构建统计假设检验来区分统计涨落与真实硬件错误。

Method: 在零假设（NSIT违反仅来自统计涨落）下构建统计假设检验；利用零假设下NSIT违反的统计性质结合切比雪夫不等式，推导出无需参数假设的p值上界。

Result: 在超导处理器单量子比特实验中，观察到NSIT违反且零假设被小p值拒绝，提供了基依赖硬件错误的统计证据。在IonQ七量子比特遥传协议中，即使平均保真度超经典阈值且非经典性度量消失时，零假设仍被拒绝。

Conclusion: 统计假设检验对于在近端量子器件中检测基依赖错误是必要的，仅凭NSIT违反或平均保真度不足以可靠识别硬件错误。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal quantum steering provides a framework for benchmarking the nonclassicality of general quantum state transfer processes. A central diagnostic is the no-signaling-in-time (NSIT) condition, whose violation can indicate basis-dependent hardware errors. However, finite measurement statistics may also yield apparent violations, thereby obscuring the detection of basis-dependent hardware errors. To address this, we construct a statistical hypothesis test under the null hypothesis that NSIT violations arise solely from statistical fluctuations. Combining the statistical properties of NSIT violation under the null hypothesis with Chebyshev's inequality, we obtain a distribution-free upper bound on the $p$-value without parametric assumptions. We apply this method to two examples. For a single-qubit state-transfer experiment on a superconducting processor, we observe several instances that the NSIT violation is observed and the null hypothesis is simultaneously rejected by a small $p$-value, providing statistical evidence of basis-dependent hardware errors. For a seven-qubit Hayden-Preskill teleportation protocol on IonQ devices, the null hypothesis is also rejected even when the average fidelity exceeds the classical threshold, while the associated nonclassicality measure vanishes. Our results highlight the necessity of statistical hypothesis testing for detecting basis-dependent errors in near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [121] [Fully Quantum Algorithm for the 1-dimensional linear Lattice Boltzmann Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16514)
*Mohammed Bediche,Matthijs van Waveren,Denis Ricot,Pierre Sagaut*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解一维线性对流-扩散方程的完全量子算法，该算法基于Lattice Boltzmann方法。相比现有的混合量子-经典算法（每时间步需测量），改进后的完全量子算法仅在算法结束时进行一次测量。算法先在量子模拟器上验证，随后在133量子比特的量子系统上运行，研究了噪声和电路深度对输出状态的影响，发现量子比特退相干噪声会导致最终结果波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子-经典Lattice Boltzmann算法每时间步都需要进行测量，导致计算效率受限且易受测量噪声干扰。本文旨在设计一种完全量子算法，仅需在算法结束时进行一次测量，以提升计算效率并降低噪声影响。

Method: 提出了一种完全量子Lattice Boltzmann算法，用于求解一维线性对流-扩散方程。该算法包含两个自由度，相比混合量子-经典方案，仅在算法结束时执行一次测量操作。算法先在量子模拟器上验证，后在133量子比特的真实量子系统上运行，以评估噪声和电路深度的影响。

Result: 量子模拟器验证了完全量子算法的正确性；在133量子比特量子系统上的实验表明，量子比特的退相干噪声会引入最终结果的波动。

Conclusion: 完全量子Lattice Boltzmann算法能有效求解一维线性对流-扩散方程，但实际量子硬件上的退相干噪声对结果精度有显著影响，未来需要进一步优化量子电路深度和噪声缓解策略。

Abstract: A fully quantum algorithm for solving the one-dimensional linear advection-diffusion equation using the Lattice Boltzmann method as a numerical procedure is presented in this work. We start by presenting a state of the art of the current usage of quantum algorithms for solving ordinary and partial differential equations. We then describe two algorithms for the one-dimensional Lattice Boltzmann method with two degrees of freedom. The first one is an existing hybrid quantum-classical algorithm with measurements at each time step, and the second one is our improved version, viz. a fully quantum algorithm where only one measurement is needed at the end of the algorithm. The fully quantum algorithm is first executed on a quantum simulator and then compared with a classical approach. Subsequently, the fully quantum algorithm is run on a quantum system with 133 qubits to investigate the effect of noise and the depth of the circuit on the output state. We find fluctuations in the final result due to the decoherence noise of the qubits.

</details>


### [122] [Phase controlled spectral topology, dynamic stability and sensitivity in Non-Hermitian Cavity Magnonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16522)
*Sachin Singh Bargahi,Rajeev Singh,Biswanath Bhoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了非厄米腔磁振子系统，通过单一可调相位控制相干光子-磁振子相互作用与耗散耦合之间的干涉，实现厄米/反厄米耦合的连续旋转、能级排斥与吸引的动态转换、非互易输运、增强传感等功能，建立了谱拓扑、定向输运、动力学稳定性和传感的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过单一的干涉相位作为普适控制参数，在不改变系统本征参数的情况下，连续调控非厄米腔磁振子系统的耦合性质（厄米/反厄米）、谱拓扑（奇异点）、输运特性（非互易）和动力学行为，并实现增强传感功能。

Method: 基于非厄米腔磁振子平台的理论分析，引入可调相位控制相干光子-磁振子相互作用与库工程耗散耦合之间的干涉，研究系统的谱拓扑、非互易输运、非平衡动力学以及奇异点增强传感。

Result: 相位可实现厄米与反厄米耦合的连续旋转，驱动能级排斥与吸引的动态转换；系统展现出奇异点、线宽工程、零阻尼条件、强非互易输运和相位可调隔离；干涉相位控制杂化模式的稳定性，实现稳定弛豫、临界慢化、振荡能量交换和指数放大动力学；奇异点响应呈现平方根标度，可用于增强传感。

Conclusion: 通过库工程干涉在腔磁振子系统中建立了统一的理论框架，将谱拓扑、定向输运、动力学稳定性和传感功能联系起来。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate a non-Hermitian cavity-magnon platform in which coherent photonmagnon interactions and reservoir-mediated dissipative coupling interfere through a single externally tunable phase. We show that this interference phase provides a universal control parameter that continuously rotates the effective coupling between Hermitian and anti-Hermitian regimes, enabling dynamic transitions between level repulsion and level attraction without modifying intrinsic system parameters. The resulting phase-controlled non-Hermitian topology gives rise to exceptional points, linewidth engineering, and zero-damping conditions. Owing to the propagation-direction dependence of the dissipative interaction, the system further exhibits strong nonreciprocal transport and phase-tunable isolation arising from asymmetric hybridization of the cavity and magnon modes. Beyond its spectral and transport properties, we establish a direct connection between nonHermitian spectral topology and nonequilibrium population dynamics. The interference phase governs the stability of the hybrid modes, driving transitions between stable relaxation, critical slowing down near exceptional points, oscillatory energy exchange, and exponentially amplified dynamics. We further demonstrate that the same phase-controlled exceptional topology can be exploited for enhanced sensing, where the eigenvalue response exhibits the characteristic square-root scaling associated with exceptional-point physics. Our results provide a unified framework linking spectral topology, directional transport, dynamical stability, and sensing functionality through reservoirengineered interference in cavity magnonic systems.

</details>


### [123] [Preparation of Fractional Quantum Hall States on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16548)
*Hao Wu,Lei-Yi-Nan Liu,Zhao-Xin Pei,Yi-Xuan Zhai,Zhen-Xu Luo,Zhao Liu,Jian Cui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于直接量子电路构建的方案，用于在任意几何结构上精确制备分数量子霍尔（FQH）态（以ν=1/3 Laughlin态在球面几何为例），相比变分量子电路方法显著减少了双量子比特门数量和电路深度，并利用最优控制技术为超导和里德伯原子平台设计了可行实验方案，为在NISQ和容错量子设备上实现FQH态提供了高效且与硬件相关的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算机上实现具有分数电荷和本征拓扑序的分数量子霍尔态是凝聚态物理与量子信息科学交叉领域的一个核心挑战。

Method: 提出一种直接量子电路构建的互补方案，适用于任意几何结构；具体以ν=1/3 Laughlin态在球面几何上的精确制备为例；采用最优控制技术为超导和里德伯原子平台设计控制脉冲。

Result: 相比变分量子电路方法，显著减少了所需双量子比特门数量和电路深度；识别出实验上可行的态制备协议。

Conclusion: 为在NISQ和容错量子设备上实现通用FQH态提供了一条高效且与硬件相关的途径。

Abstract: The realization of fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states, characterized by fractional charge and intrinsic topological order, on quantum computers represents a central challenge at the interface of condensed matter physics and quantum information science. Current methods are grouped into two types: methods based on (quasi-)adiabatic evolution of complex parent Hamiltonians to yield target states, and circuit-based approaches for direct state preparation, which are confined to effectively one-dimensional systems near the thin cylinder or torus limit. We introduce a complementary scheme relying on direct quantum circuit construction, which works for arbitrary geometries. Specifically, we present a method to precisely prepare the $ν=1/3$ Laughlin state on the sphere geometry and demonstrate that it significantly reduces the required number of two-qubit gates and circuit depth, compared to variational quantum circuit approaches. In addition, we employ optimal control techniques to design control pulses for both superconducting and Rydberg atom platforms, identifying experimentally feasible protocols for state preparation. Our results provide an efficient and hardware-relevant pathway for realizing generic FQH states on both noisy intermediate-scale and fault-tolerant quantum devices.

</details>


### [124] [What does measuring one qubit reveal about another? $K$-networks as a directed diagnostic for quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16549)
*Kostas Blekos,Paulo Vitor Itaboraí*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有向、基依赖的边权 $K_{i\to j}$，用于量化测量一个量子比特后对另一个量子比特条件状态的改变强度，并可基于两比特约化密度矩阵计算，生成电路状态的有向网络层。与对称关联度量不同，$K$ 网络能捕获前馈、相位和交互图结构，且在纯两比特态下约化为纠缠度量 $C^2$，而可分离混合态也可达到 $K=1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多量子比特电路状态难以直接检查，常用成对图权重来总结，但现有对称相关性度量无法回答有向、基依赖的问题：若以某基测量量子比特 $i$，其结果如何重塑量子比特 $j$ 的条件状态？需要一种有向、基条件化的边权来回答该问题。

Method: 定义 $K_{i\to j}$ 为有向、基条件化的边权，利用标准二元系综可区分性度量，将条件比较转化为电路状态的有向网络层。该权重可从两比特约化密度矩阵计算，当两个测量结果以可比概率出现且使目标比特处于明显不同的条件状态时较大，当源结果确定或目标状态不可区分时为零。

Result: 对于纯两比特态，$K$ 约化为纠缠度量 $C^2$（平方并发度）；可分离混合态也可达到 $K=1$。在量子隐形传态、Grover 算法、QAOA 和随机电路系列上的示例表明，$K$ 网络能映射出前馈、相位和交互图结构，而这些结构在对称或计算基摘要中可能很弱或不存在。

Conclusion: $K$ 网络是一种诊断工具（而非纠缠度量），能有效揭示电路状态中对称度量难以捕获的有向和基依赖的结构信息，为多量子比特电路提供新的分析视角。

Abstract: Many-qubit circuit states are hard to inspect directly, so they are often summarized by pairwise graph weights. Common pairwise weights report symmetric correlations, while many circuit questions are directed and basis-specific: if qubit $i$ is measured in a given basis, how strongly does the outcome reshape the conditional state of qubit $j$? We define $K_{i\to j}$, a directed, basis-conditioned edge weight for this question. It is large when the two measurement outcomes occur with comparable probability and leave qubit $j$ in clearly different conditional states; it is zero when the source outcome is deterministic or the target states are indistinguishable. The scalar uses standard binary-ensemble distinguishability; the paper's contribution is to turn this conditional comparison into a directed network layer for circuit states. The resulting networks are computable from two-qubit reduced density matrices. They are diagnostic (not entanglement measures): for pure two-qubit states $K$ reduces to the tangle $C^2$ (squared concurrence)~\cite{WoottersConcurrence,CKWTangle}, while separable mixed states can reach $K=1$. Examples on teleportation, Grover, QAOA, and random circuit families show the intended use: $K$-networks map feed-forward, phase, and interaction-graph structure that symmetric or computational-basis summaries can leave weak or absent.

</details>


### [125] [Electronic Band Structure of Silicon Determined via a Variational Adiabatic Eigensolver: Theory and Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16604)
*Xingrui Liu,Liyang Sui,Tianqi Cai,Zhiwen Zong,Kunliang Bu,Wenyan Jin,Bowen Chen,Xutao Zhang,Yufan Li,Zhihao Gong,Yicong Zheng,Shengyu Zhang,Jianlan Wu,Yi Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分绝热本征求解器(VAE)协议，结合绝热演化与变分优化，在NISQ设备上高效制备半导体能带结构的高保真激发态。通过对硅的电子能带结构进行数值模拟和超导量子处理器实验，实现了超过96%的态保真度，能量偏差小于0.5 eV，进一步优化后低于0.01 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决半导体能带结构计算中激发态制备的关键挑战，克服传统绝热方法电路构建困难以及变分量子本征求解器在激发态精度不足的局限性。

Method: 提出VAE协议，结合绝热演化与变分优化；采用动量空间截断减少量子比特需求；使用多量子比特参数化电路和基于相位的损失函数；通过数值模拟和超导量子处理器实验验证。

Result: 单次读取态保真度超过96%；测量能量期望与理论能带能量偏差在0.5 eV以内；通过单频率振荡拟合进一步将能量偏差降至0.01 eV以下。

Conclusion: 该框架为精确确定量子材料电子结构提供了稳健且实用的途径。

Abstract: This work addresses the critical challenge of excited-state preparation for semiconductor band structure calculations. We introduce a variational adiabatic eigensolver (VAE) protocol that combines adiabatic evolution with variational optimization to prepare high-fidelity eigenstates on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices. Applying a momentum-space truncation, we accurately compute the electronic band structure of silicon -- an idealized infinite periodic system -- using only a modest number of qubits. Our approach employs multi-qubit parameterized circuits and a phase-based loss function, overcoming limitations of conventional methods. These limitations include the circuit-construction difficulty in traditional adiabatic approaches and the reduced accuracy of variational quantum eigensolvers for excited states. Through rigorous numerical simulation and experimental implementation on a superconducting quantum processor, we successfully prepare silicon's valence-band and conduction-band eigenstates. Single-shot readout yields state fidelities exceeding 96%, and the measured energy expectations agree with theoretical band energies within 0.5 eV. Further refinement via single-frequency oscillation fitting reduces the energy deviation to below 0.01 eV. This framework provides a robust and practical pathway for precisely determining electronic structures in quantum materials.

</details>


### [126] [Fuzzy-processing quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16623)
*Yan-Xiong Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与模糊处理相结合的新型量子计算框架，通过模糊状态识别、模糊编码和模糊反馈三方面优势，降低了对门保真度的要求、抑制了长量子电路的输出波动，并能在缺乏精确状态信息时高效控制量子比特，且可与量子纠错集成，适用于决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计算面临退相干和控制误差的挑战，现有的量子纠错方法需要大量冗余量子比特、精确测量和及时反馈，成本高昂且实现困难。因此需要探索新的量子计算框架以降低这些要求。

Method: 提出模糊处理框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 模糊识别——降低量子态识别精度要求，从而降低所需门保真度；(2) 模糊编码——将量子比特信息编码为概率分布，抑制长量子电路输出的波动；(3) 模糊反馈——在缺乏量子态精确信息时提供更高效的控制方式。该框架还可集成到量子纠错中，消除即时纠错操作的需求。

Result: 该模糊处理框架在三个方面带来显著收益：降低门保真度要求、抑制长电路输出波动、实现无精确信息下的高效控制。同时可与量子纠错集成，减少即时纠错需求。

Conclusion: 该方案特别适用于决策问题的求解，在优化问题和控制问题中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum computation has attracted numerous attentions and develops rapidly in the recent decades. To against the decoherence and the control errors upon the qubits, quantum error corrections are adopted. Such approaches require lots of redundant qubits, accurate measurement and timely feedback. Here we investigate a new framework of quantum computation that is associated with fuzzy processing. It will benefit significantly from three aspects: the fuzzy recognition of qubit states reduce the required gate fidelity; the fuzzy encoding encodes the information of the qubits into a distribution of probability, suppressing the fluctuations in the output of long quantum circuits; the fuzzy feedback offers a more efficient way to control the qubits when precision information of quantum states are absent. Furthermore, the fuzzy processing can be integrated into quantum error correction, eliminating the need for immediate correction operations. The proposed scheme will be fairly suitable for the solution of decision problems, which has significant applications in the optimization problems and control problems.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum enhancement and Doppler suppression of Kasevich-Chu atom interferometer with motional squeezing states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16632)
*Dongyang Yu,Yubin Wang,Fong En Oon,Qiang Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统推导了Kasevich-Chu干涉仪中内外自由度杂化框架下的量子与经典Fisher信息，揭示了动量压缩态输入对Doppler效应抑制的鲁棒性，并发现通过扩大原子相干长度，布居数测量的灵敏度可达半经典极限的四倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Kasevich-Chu干涉仪受Doppler效应导致的退相干限制，而内部自旋压缩在移动平台中易受噪声破坏。本文旨在探索利用外部动量压缩态（运动压缩态）来增强干涉仪灵敏度，并系统分析其对Doppler效应的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于内外自由度杂化的Kasevich-Chu干涉仪模型，引入运动压缩态作为输入，系统推导了两种测量协议（布居数测量、联合布居数与位置测量）的量子Fisher信息和经典Fisher信息计算框架。通过最大化经典Fisher信息获取最优控制参数及对应的量子Fisher信息。

Result: 布居数测量中，通过扩大原子相干长度，最大灵敏度可达半经典极限的四倍。联合测量中，存在三种行为区域：量子增强主导区（即使强Doppler展宽下灵敏度仍显著增强）、最优压缩参数区（经典Fisher信息达到最大）以及竞争区。

Conclusion: 外部量子增强对Doppler抑制具有鲁棒性，该方案可应用于移动平台重力仪，避免内部自旋纠缠易受噪声破坏的问题。

Abstract: Hybridization of internal and external atomic degrees of freedom in a Kasevich-Chu interferometer enables the possibility to enhance the sensitivity significantly even under quantum-standard limit. By introducing motional squeezing state as an input, we systematically derive the computational framework of quantum and classical Fisher information of two measurement protocols for arbitrary strength of Doppler effects. Through maximizing the corresponding classical Fisher information, we obtain the optimal control parameters and the corresponding quantum Fisher information. For population measurement, the largest sensitivity can be as large as four times than the semi-classical limit through enlarging the atom coherence length. For joint measurement of population and position, the competition between quantum enhancement and Doppler suppression induces two three behaviors, in one regime, the quantum enhancement dominates even in presence of strong Doppler broadening effects where the sensitivity is significantly enhanced; while in another regime, an optimal squeezing parameter is observed where the classical Fisher information reaches the maximum. Our results clearly demonstrate the robustness of external quantum enhancement against Doppler suppression. Our proposal can be readily applied to gravimeter of mobile platform where decoherence from noise will damage the many-body entanglement of internal spin squeezing.

</details>


### [128] [Ultrastrongly coupled open systems and fine grained time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16634)
*Stefano Marcantoni,Marco Merkli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了d级量子系统在超强耦合玻色子库下的动力学过程，通过引入与耦合强度倒数成比例的精细缩放时间尺度，严格推导了开放系统动力学，揭示了退相干如何在新时间尺度上连续展开，并证明了非半群形式的马尔可夫动力学自然出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限耦合强度极限下，系统会经历瞬时非选择性测量和幺正芝诺动力学，但这一动态过程的中间细节尚不明确。作者希望通过引入精细缩放时间尺度来解析这一过程。

Method: 引入与耦合强度倒数成比例的精细缩放时间尺度（短时间尺度），在超强耦合区域对开放系统动力学进行严格推导。

Result: 退相干在新时间尺度上连续展开；产生了非半群形式的马尔可夫动力学，这与弱耦合理论中的情况形成对比。

Conclusion: 超强耦合区域存在独特的动力学行为，退相干过程可在精细时间尺度上被解析，且马尔可夫动力学不限于半群形式。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a d-level quantum system coupled to a bosonic reservoir when the coupling constant is large. It is known that in the limit of infinite coupling strength, the system undergoes an instantaneous nonselective measurement, resulting in the immediate decoherence in the measurement basis, followed by a unitary Zeno dynamics. Here we resolve this dynamical process by introducing a fine grained scaling regime of short times proportional to the inverse coupling. We provide a rigorous derivation of the open system dynamics in this regime of ultrastrong coupling and demonstrate how decoherence unfolds continuously in the new time scale. We show that Markovian dynamics which are not given by semigroups arise naturally, in contrast to what happens in the weak coupling theory.

</details>


### [129] [High-dimensional coherence to entanglement transduction under canonical noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16695)
*Asad Ali,Aiham M. Rostom,Saif Al-Kuwari,H. Kuniyil,M. T. Rahim,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了高维双粒子量子系统（qunit）中相干性到纠缠转换的分析框架，通过广义受控移位操作将相干输入qunit与不相干辅助粒子耦合产生最大关联态，并研究了三种噪声（相位阻尼、全局退极化、独立振幅阻尼）对转换后纠缠的影响，揭示了不同噪声的退化特性，包括均匀衰减、纠缠突然死亡的维度依赖阈值和非对称衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维量子系统中，相干性与纠缠是两种关键量子资源，理解它们之间的转换机制及其在噪声环境下的行为，对于量子信息处理具有重要意义。作者旨在建立精确且维度无关的解析框架，为高维资源转换和噪声纠缠生成提供理论基准。

Method: 通过广义受控移位操作将任意相干输入qunit与不相干辅助粒子耦合，产生最大关联的双粒子态；利用输出态的部分转置分析，建立输入相干性与生成纠缠之间的精确维度无关联系；分别研究相位阻尼、全局退极化噪声和独立振幅阻尼三种噪声过程对转换后纠缠的影响，推导解析表达式。

Result: 三种噪声以不同方式退化解纠缠：相位阻尼导致均匀衰减、退极化噪声引入与纠缠突然死亡相关的成对阈值、振幅阻尼产生朝向基态弛豫的非对称衰减。对于最大相干输入，结果简化为封闭形式，可直观比较三种噪声机制随维度增加的行为，其中退极化噪声展现出维度依赖的突然死亡阈值，振幅阻尼则为平滑抑制。

Conclusion: 该分析框架为高维资源转换提供了有用的解析基准，对基于qudit的量子信息设置中评估噪声纠缠生成具有实际价值。

Abstract: We develop an analytical framework for coherence-to-entanglement conversion in bipartite high-dimensional quantum systems, so-called qunits. An arbitrary coherent input qunit is coupled to an incoherent ancilla through a generalized controlled-shift operation, producing a maximally correlated bipartite state. By analyzing the partial transpose of the output state, we establish an exact dimension-independent connection between the input coherence and the generated entanglement. We then study how this conversion is affected by three standard noise processes applied after the conversion step: phase damping, global depolarizing noise, and independent amplitude damping. The resulting expressions show that these channels degrade entanglement in qualitatively different ways. Phase damping leads to a uniform attenuation of the entanglement generated from coherence, depolarizing noise introduces pairwise thresholds associated with entanglement sudden death, and amplitude damping produces an asymmetric decay governed by relaxation toward the ground state. For maximally coherent inputs, the general results reduce to simple closed-form behavior, allowing direct comparison of the three noise mechanisms as the system dimension increases. In particular, global depolarizing noise exhibits a dimension-dependent sudden-death threshold, while amplitude damping leads to a smooth suppression in the maximally coherent case. These results provide useful analytical benchmarks for high-dimensional resource conversion and for assessing noisy entanglement generation in qudit-based quantum-information settings.

</details>


### [130] [Complete entanglement detection using polynomial invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16712)
*Thomas C. Fraser,Vjosa Blakaj,Roberto Rubboli,Felix Huber,Marco Fanizza*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，通过推导可分态张量幂的普适界限和构造完整的非线性纠缠见证族，同时克服了现有纠缠检测方法中数值优化需求与不完备性的两大局限，实现了无需显式密度矩阵即可检测所有形式纠缠，且具有基无关（局部酉不变）特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠检测方法存在两类局限：一是完备但需要显式密度矩阵并进行数值优化；二是可直接测量但不完备，无法检测所有形式的纠缠。本文旨在克服这两类局限，建立统一框架。

Method: 首先，通过推导可分态张量幂的普适界限来绕过数值优化，并证明这些界限是完备的（每个纠缠态在足够大的张量幂下都会违反这些界限）。其次，显式构造一个对应的完备非线性纠缠见证族，可在无需显式密度矩阵的情况下检测所有形式的纠缠，且该见证具有基无关性（局部酉变换下不变）。

Result: 实现了无需数值优化和显式密度矩阵即可完备检测所有形式纠缠；构造的纠缠见证具有局部酉不变性；将纠缠检测工具扩展到任意局域维度。

Conclusion: 该工作以流形不变的方式扩展了任意局域维度下的纠缠检测工具箱，统一解决了完备性与可操作性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Existing methods for deciding whether a bipartite quantum state is separable or entangled typically fall into one of two categories: they are either complete but require access to an explicit density matrix followed by numerical optimization, or they can be evaluated directly by measuring the quantum system but are incomplete, in the sense that they cannot detect all forms of entanglement. In this work, we overcome both limitations in a unified framework. First, we bypass numerical optimization by deriving separability criteria in the form of universal bounds on tensor powers of separable states. We prove that these bounds are complete: every entangled state violates them for sufficiently large tensor powers.
