Second, we explicitly construct a corresponding complete family of nonlinear entanglement witnesses, which can detect all forms of entanglement without requiring an explicit density matrix. The witnesses we construct are moreover basis-independent, in the sense that they are invariant under conjugation by local unitaries. Altogether, our results expand the toolbox for entanglement detection in arbitrary local dimensions in a manifestly invariant way.

</details>


### [131] [Scalable generation of heralded single photons via active feed-forward switching of a fiber delay line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16741)
*Xavier Barcons Planas,Helen M. Chrzanowski,Janik Wolters*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用自由空间-光纤混合延迟线和前馈控制技术，实现了基于SPDC的准确定性单光子源的时间同步，在20个时间箱上实现六倍增强的同步速率和多光子同步，且高阶光子数贡献仅略有增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPDC光子源的概率性本质限制了同步多光子态的生成，亟需实现多个光子生成事件的时间同步以支持光子量子技术。

Method: 采用自由空间-光纤混合延迟线结合前馈控制，实现快速高效的可切换操作；从单片腔SPDC源产生窄带、电信波长光子，并在20个时间箱上进行同步。

Result: 同步速率提升了六倍，实现了多光子同步，且高阶光子数贡献仅略有增加。

Conclusion: 该方案有效提升了SPDC光子源的确定性程度，为可扩展的光子量子技术提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Quasi-deterministic single-photon generation is a key requirement for many photonic quantum technologies. Photon sources based on spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) are widely used for producing high-quality photons; however, the probabilistic nature of the process limits the generation of synchronized multi-photon states. Here, we demonstrate temporal synchronization of multiple photon-generation events using a free-space-fiber hybrid delay line with feed-forward control, enabling fast and efficient switching and scalable operation. Narrow-band, telecom-wavelength photons compatible for fiber transmission are heralded from a monolithic cavity SPDC source and synchronized across 20 time bins. This yields a sixfold enhancement in synchronized rates and enables multi-photon synchronization, with only a marginal increase of higher-order photon-number contributions.

</details>


### [132] [Charging Quantum Batteries with Chiral Squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16764)
*Borhan Ahmadi,André H. A. Malavazi,Janine Splettstoesser,Paweł Horodecki,Lei Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于驱动玻色子Kitaev链的量子电池充电方案，通过手性压缩将被动输入涨落转化为有序的非被动电池态，在热噪声和对称性保持无序下实现接近单位的工作信噪比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用手性压缩将被动输入涨落高效转化为可提取的电池能量，克服传统相位保持放大器在储能方面的局限性。

Method: 基于驱动玻色子Kitaev链，利用手性压缩机制处理相干输入脉冲和双向传播的涨落，在链两端正交方向上对涨落进行放大和压缩。

Result: 实现了大量可提取能量的存储，在存在热噪声和中等对称性保持无序的情况下，工作信噪比接近单位（1）。

Conclusion: 该量子电池充电方案在抗噪声和抗无序方面表现稳健，相较传统放大器在储能效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-battery charger based on a driven bosonic Kitaev chain (BKC), where chiral squeezing converts passive input fluctuations into ordered, non-passive battery states. While a coherent input pulse exhibits phase-sensitive chiral transport, the charging dynamics is dominated by bidirectionally propagating fluctuations that are amplified and squeezed into orthogonal quadratures at opposite chain ends. In contrast to conventional phase-preserving amplifiers, our scheme stores largely extractable energy and achieves a work-like signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) near unity, even in the presence of thermal noise and moderate symmetry-preserving disorder.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum Nonlocal Games on Graph Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16784)
*Joshua Tucker,Chris Weeks,Peter Drmota,Ellis M. Ainley,Ayush Agrawal,Adam R. Martinez,Erin Malinowski,Jacob A. Blackmore,David P. Nadlinger,Gabriel Araneda,David M. Lucas,Carlos A. Perez-Delgado,Paul Strange,Jorge Quintanilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过理论发展和实验验证，展示了量子纠缠在移动代理空间协调任务中的实际优势。研究突破了以往高度理想化的条件限制，开发了处理地形不确定性的图集成理论，并在分离的离子阱系统间进行了实验验证，同时在超导硬件上模拟了更广泛的问题。研究发现，代理获取更多局部信息时量子优势反而增加，这是经典方法无法实现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究证明量子纠缠可使空间分离的代理在无需通信的情况下实现经典手段不可能的协调行动，但相关演示依赖于高度理想化条件——代理需完全掌握地形信息，且实验仅限于单量子处理器内的模拟。本研究旨在解决这两个限制，探索在真实不确定环境中的量子优势。

Method: 1) 发展图集成理论以刻画地形不确定性；2) 在物理分离的离子阱系统间通过远程纠缠进行会合场景实验验证；3) 在超导硬件上模拟更广泛的问题集。

Result: 在会合场景中实现了量子优势的实验验证；发现代理获取更多局部信息时量子优势反而增大，这与经典直觉相悖。

Conclusion: 研究为运动协调问题中实现实用量子优势建立了具体路径，并指出了利用便携式量子设备在不确定环境中增强集体决策的新方向。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is one of the most striking discoveries in all of science. This effect allows, for instance, two spatially separated agents to coordinate their actions, without communication, to an extent that is both counter-intuitive, and provably impossible by any other physical means. A recently discovered example is that of mobile agents (players) performing spatial coordination tasks such as rendezvous, where the agents aim to meet on a network without communication. Until now, demonstrations of this advantage have relied on highly idealized conditions: agents are assumed to have complete knowledge of the topography, and experiments have been restricted to simulations using data generated by qubits within a single quantum processor. Here we address both limitations by developing a theory for graph ensembles that capture topographical uncertainty and by experimentally demonstrating the advantage in rendezvous scenarios between physically separated ion-trap systems with access to remote entanglement. Moreover, we simulate a broader set of problems on superconducting hardware. Surprisingly, when players are given the ability to gather more local information the quantum advantage increases -- a feat impossible by classical means. Our findings establish a concrete route toward practical quantum advantages in motion coordination problems. More broadly, they point to a new way of using portable quantum devices to enhance collective decision-making in uncertain environments.

</details>


### [134] [Physically Motivated Ansatz for Open Fermionic Systems on Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16823)
*Yi Liu,Xiaopeng Li,Zhen Liu,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NE-UCC的物理启发式变分拟设，用于求解开放费米子系统的非平衡稳态（NESS），相比硬件高效拟设（HEA）在强关联非平衡区域将保真度误差降低多达十个数量级，并支持具有特定对称性的激发本征模探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定开放费米子系统的非平衡稳态（NESS）是一个基础性问题，类似于闭系统的基态求解。尽管变分量子算法可用于求解Lindblad主方程，但现有的NESS拟设设计仍面临困难，主要依赖的硬件高效拟设（HEA）存在贫瘠高原问题。

Method: 引入了一种物理启发的变分拟设NE-UCC（Non-Equilibrium Unitary Coupled Cluster），用于变分求解Lindblad主方程以获取非平衡稳态。

Result: 数值模拟表明，即使在远离平衡的强关联区域，NE-UCC也能可靠收敛到稳态；与HEA相比，保真度误差降低多达十个数量级；此外，NE-UCC还能促进具有特定对称性的激发本征模的探索。

Conclusion: NE-UCC作为一种物理驱动的拟设，在求解开放费米子系统NESS方面显著优于HEA，克服了贫瘠高原问题，为变分量子算法在开放量子系统中的应用提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Determining non-equilibrium steady states (NESS) of open fermionic systems is a fundamental problem akin to finding ground states of closed systems. To address this, variational quantum algorithms can be used to solve the Lindblad master equation, much like the Schrödinger equation, yet ansatz design for NESS remains challenging. Existing approaches rely mostly on hardware-efficient ansätze (HEA), which suffer from the barren plateau problem. Here, we introduce a physically motivated ansatz named NE-UCC. Numerical simulations demonstrate that NE-UCC reliably converges to the steady state even in strongly correlated regimes far from equilibrium, reducing the infidelity by up to ten orders of magnitude compared to HEA. Furthermore, NE-UCC facilitates the exploration of excited eigenmodes with specific symmetries.

</details>


### [135] [Morphology-resolved scrambling in a chaotic quantum billiard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16865)
*Pranaya Pratik Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在花生形量子台球中建立了疤痕本征态与本征态分辨OTOC之间的形态学联系，发现强形态重复的本征态具有几乎相同的OTOC增长和饱和行为，表明疤痕结构可作为算子增长的空间模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌量子系统中疤痕本征态能保留空间记忆，但这些静态结构是否控制量子搅乱（scrambling）动力学尚不清楚，现有标量诊断方法（如微分熵、连续参与比）虽能检测反常集中性但丢失了空间架构信息。

Method: 提出尺度归一化密度重叠方法直接比较概率密度轮廓，识别具有几乎相同空间形态的正交本征态家族，并通过比较这些正交本征态的完整OTOC时间迹来建立形态与动力学的联系。

Result: 中等密度重叠无法给出普适预测，而强形态重复的本征态表现出几乎相同的OTOC增长和饱和行为，证明了形态复发具有动力学内容。

Conclusion: 疤痕结构不仅是对遍历性的静态违背，而且作为算子增长的空间模板发挥作用；该形态学分辨框架将本征态形状转化为量子搅乱的定量预测器，为弱遍历性破缺提供了尺度受控的诊断工具。

Abstract: Chaotic quantum systems can retain spatial memory through scarred eigenstates, but whether these static structures control scrambling remains unclear. This work establishes a morphology-resolved connection between scarred eigenstates and eigenstate-resolved OTOCs in a peanut-shaped quantum billiard. Scalar localisation diagnostics, including differential entropy and continuum participation ratios, detect anomalous concentration but discard spatial architecture. A scale-normalised density overlap, in contrast, directly compares probability density profiles, revealing families of orthogonal eigenstates with nearly identical spatial morphology. Comparing the complete OTOC time traces of these orthogonal eigenstates reveals that morphological recurrence has dynamical content: moderate density overlap yields no universal prediction, whereas strongly recurring morphologies exhibit nearly identical OTOC growth and saturation. Thus, scarred structures act as spatial templates for operator growth, not merely static violations of ergodicity. This morphology-resolved framework turns eigenstate shape into a quantitative predictor of scrambling and provides a scale-controlled diagnostic of weak ergodicity breaking in quantum chaos.

</details>


### [136] [Quantifying Coherence-to-Entanglement Conversion Efficiency under Noisy Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16916)
*Asad Ali,H. Kuniyil,M. I. Hussain,M. T. Rahim,Abdallah Slaoui,Saif Al-Kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在最小双量子比特协议中，局域量子相干性在有噪环境下转化为两体纠缠的极限，建立了无噪极限下输入相干性与输出纠缠的精确闭合解析关系，并在两种代表性噪声（独立相位阻尼和全局退极化噪声）下推导了纠缠存活率与转化效率的解析表达式，揭示了截然不同的退化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有噪环境中，局域量子相干性能否以及如何高效转化为两体纠缠，揭示不同噪声机制对相干-纠缠转化过程的约束，为近量子设备中的量子信息协议提供解析基准。

Method: 采用$l_1$-范数相干性和纠缠负度作为资源量化指标，在包含相干单量子比特输入、非相干辅助比特、理想CNOT操作及后续环境噪声的最小协议中，通过解析推导建立相干-纠缠对应关系，并结合密度矩阵直接模拟验证。

Result: 无噪极限下输出纠缠负度恰好为初始$l_1$-相干性的一半。相位阻尼导致通用乘法抑制，转化效率与相干性无关，无有限噪声纠缠猝死；全局退极化引入各向同性混合成分，产生相干性依赖的退化和有限猝死阈值。最大相干输入不仅最大化纠缠产生，还优化了对退极化噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估不同噪声机制如何约束基础量子信息协议中的相干-纠缠转化提供了紧凑的解析基准，对近量子器件的设计与优化具有指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate the noise-limited conversion of local quantum coherence into bipartite entanglement in a minimal two-qubit protocol comprising a coherent single-qubit input, an incoherent ancilla, an ideal CNOT operation, and subsequent environmental noise. Employing the $l_1$-norm of coherence and the entanglement negativity as resource quantifiers, we establish an exact closed-form correspondence between local single-qubit input coherence and the two-qubit entanglement generated in the noiseless limit, showing that the output negativity is precisely one half of the initial $l_1$-coherence. We then derive analytic expressions for the surviving entanglement and the associated coherence-to-entanglement conversion efficiency under two representative noise mechanisms: independent phase damping and global two-qubit depolarizing noise. The two channels exhibit qualitatively distinct degradation behavior. Phase damping induces a universal multiplicative suppression of the generated entanglement, yielding a coherence-independent conversion efficiency and no finite-noise entanglement sudden death. In contrast, global depolarization introduces an isotropic mixing contribution that shifts the partial-transpose spectrum, producing coherence-dependent degradation and a finite sudden-death threshold. We show that maximally coherent inputs not only maximize the entanglement generated by the CNOT protocol but also optimize its robustness against depolarizing noise. Direct density-matrix simulations validate the analytic results to numerical precision. These findings provide a compact analytic benchmark for assessing how different noise mechanisms constrain coherence-to-entanglement conversion in elementary quantum-information protocols and near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [137] [Controlled Quantum Metrology with Anisotropic Heisenberg Spin Interactions under Intrinsic Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16918)
*S. K. Singh,Jia-Xin Peng,Y-J Zhu,Mohammad Khalid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和内在退相干的各向异性海森堡自旋系统中的量子参数估计，发现通过调节交换各向异性和初始纠缠态可以显著提升估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在存在内在退相干（Milburn模型）的情况下，如何利用各向异性海森堡自旋系统中的DM相互作用实现高精度量子参数估计，并理解量子资源（纠缠、相干性等）在计量学中的作用。

Method: 基于量子Fisher信息（QFI）理论，推导了含时演化密度矩阵的解析表达式；分析了交换各向异性、内在退相干和探针态制备对估计精度的影响；并利用 concurrence、量子相干性和von Neumann熵辅助分析量子资源的作用。

Result: 合适的交换各向异性耦合和初始纠缠态能显著提升估计性能；均匀磁场和DM相互作用强度的估计存在不同的最优参数区间；即使在内在退相干存在下，系统仍能实现高精度量子计量。

Conclusion: 各向异性海森堡自旋系统结合DM相互作用为高精度量子计量提供了一个灵活且有前景的平台。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum parameter estimation in a two-qubit anisotropic Heisenberg spin system with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction in the presence of intrinsic decoherence described by the Milburn model. Using the Quantum Fisher Information (QFI), we study the estimation of both the uniform magnetic field and the DM interaction strength. Analytical expressions for the time-evolved density matrix are obtained and used to explore the effects of exchange anisotropy, intrinsic decoherence, and probe-state preparation on the achievable estimation precision. Our results show that suitable tuning of the anisotropic exchange coupling and the initial entangled state can considerably enhance the estimation performance, with different optimal parameter regimes emerging for magnetic-field and DM-interaction sensing. To better understand the role of quantum resources in metrology, we also examine the behaviour of concurrence, quantum coherence, and von Neumann entropy. Overall, our findings demonstrate that anisotropic Heisenberg spin systems with DM interaction provide a promising and flexible platform for high-precision quantum metrology even in the presence of intrinsic decoherence.

</details>


### [138] [Experimental quantum state learning with pairs of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16932)
*C. Pria Dobney,Johan Henaff,Allen Kasum,Rui Jie Tang,Haru Mukumoto,Mark Hillery,Berthold-Georg Englert,Aephraim Steinberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验展示了Agarwal等人提出的“成对学习”协议：通过配对到达的光子，不仅能够估计量子态的密度矩阵，还能唯一地分解出混合态中的两个纯态成分及其权重，仅需约10^4个光子即可达到0.9999的断层扫描保真度，并能区分夹角小于5度的不同纯态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常密度矩阵无法唯一分解为纯态分量，Agarwal等人提出了一种协议，当量子比特以成对方式到达（每对中两个量子比特处于相同状态）时，可以推断出混合态中具体的纯态成分及其权重。本文旨在实验验证这一概念。

Method: 利用光子偏振自由度，通过断层扫描测量单光子序列，利用到达时间信息将光子配对。基于配对信息，推断光子的偏振态及其概率。针对不同偏振态选择和比例进行了实验，并研究了区分两个具有相同混合态但由不同正交偏振态对构成的等比例混合物的能力。

Result: 约10^4个光子足以达到约0.9999的断层扫描保真度，足以区分由夹角小于5度的纯态制备的两种相同混合态。

Conclusion: 成对到达信息可以有效解决密度矩阵分解的非唯一性问题，实验验证了该协议的有效性，并展示了其在区分相似混合态制备中的高灵敏度。

Abstract: Tomography allows one to estimate the density matrix describing the state an ensemble of quantum systems are prepared in (for example, polarization tomography determines the polarization state of a beam of identically prepared photons). In general, it is not possible to uniquely decompose the density matrix into its pure state components. Agarwal et al. proposed a protocol which, for a mixture composed of any two pure states of a qubit (with arbitrary probabilities), allows an observer to infer not only the density matrix but the identity of those specific pure states and their weights - the additional requirement being that the qubits arrive in pairs, where both qubits in each pair are in the same state. We experimentally demonstrate this learning-from-pairs concept using photons in the polarization degree of freedom. We use tomography to measure a sequence of single photons and make use of their time-of-arrival information to 'pair up' the photons after the measurement. From here we are able to infer the photons' polarization states and their respective probabilities, and we demonstrate this for various different choices of polarization states and ratios. Finally, we investigate our ability to discriminate between two equal mixtures of distinct pairs of orthogonal polarization states. We find that on the order of approx. 10e4 photons is typically enough to achieve tomography fidelities of approximately 0.9999. This is sufficient to discriminate between two different preparations of the same mixed state, differing by angles of less than 5 degrees between the pure states used in the two preparations.

</details>


### [139] [Counterdiabatic Raman Atom Optics for Compact High-Sensitivity Gravimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16945)
*Asad Ali,Hamid Arian Zad,Saif Al-Kuwari,Muhammad Irtiza Hussain,Muhammad Talha Rahim,Hashir Kuniyil,Tim Byrnes,James Q. Quach,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了受激拉曼超绝热捷径（STIRSAP）在 Mach-Zehnder 干涉仪中实现高保真度大动量转移（LMT）原子光学。通过将反绝热修正直接编码到拉曼脉冲包络中，无需辅助控制场，数值模拟显示单脉冲转移保真度达 0.99902，并识别出在 n≈270 处存在无约束散粒噪声最优值，指出极端 LMT 阶数的实际操作受波包分离、振动噪声等多因素制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMT 原子干涉仪虽能提升惯性灵敏度，但长拉曼脉冲序列中脉冲转移误差的累积严重限制了其可扩展性，亟需一种高保真度、低脉冲时间开销的原子光学方案以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用受激拉曼超绝热捷径（STIRSAP）理论框架，将反绝热修正直接编码于拉曼脉冲包络中，基于有效拉曼模型进行数值模拟，分析脉冲保真度、干涉对比度衰减与相位增强之间的权衡关系。

Result: 1 μs 的 STIRSAP 脉冲实现单脉冲转移保真度 Fπ=0.99902；识别出无约束散粒噪声最优值在 n≈270 附近；极端 LMT 阶数的实际限制来自波包分离、振动噪声、多普勒失谐和累积系统效应，而非脉冲持续时间本身。

Conclusion: 超绝热拉曼控制是实现可扩展高保真度原子光学的一种有前景的方法，并揭示了紧凑型高阶原子干涉仪的基本物理限制。

Abstract: Large-momentum-transfer (LMT) atom interferometry provides a route toward enhanced inertial sensitivity in compact quantum sensors, but its scalability is limited by the accumulation of pulse-transfer errors across long Raman pulse sequences. We investigate theoretically the use of stimulated Raman shortcut-to-adiabatic passage (STIRSAP) for high-fidelity LMT atom optics in a Mach--Zehnder interferometer geometry. The counterdiabatic correction is encoded directly into the Raman pulse envelopes, eliminating the need for auxiliary microwave or radio-frequency control fields. Numerical simulations based on an effective Raman model show that $1~μ\mathrm{s}$ STIRSAP pulses achieve single-pulse transfer fidelities of $F_π= 0.99902$ while maintaining negligible pulse-time overhead even at high momentum order. We analyze the resulting tradeoff between interferometric phase enhancement and compound contrast decay and identify an unconstrained shot-noise optimum near $n\approx270$. The analysis further shows that practical operation at extreme LMT order is constrained by wave-packet separation, vibration noise, Doppler detuning, and accumulated systematic effects rather than by pulse duration itself. These results establish superadiabatic Raman control as a promising approach for scalable high-fidelity atom optics and clarify the physical limitations governing compact high-order atom interferometers.

</details>


### [140] [The Optimal Rate Function in Covariant Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16948)
*Arick Grootveld,Alexander Maloney,Jason Pollack,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子层析协议的最优性，证明了协变层析协议中Keyl的Schur采样协议具有最大速率函数，该速率函数是量子相对熵的退火版本，并构建了达到量子相对熵界的协议族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子层析旨在从多个副本的测量中估计未知量子态ρ。核心问题是如何选择多副本测量协议以获得最优估计，即通过速率函数刻画协议指数级收敛速度，并寻找能达到最大速率函数的最优协议。

Method: 通过速率函数（量子相对熵的推广）刻画协议性能，证明其被量子相对熵D(σ∥ρ)上界约束，并构建一族协议使速率函数收敛于该上界。针对协变层析协议（基于基无关的状态估计），证明Keyl的Schur采样协议在所有协变协议中具有最大速率函数。

Result: 证明了量子相对熵界是紧的：存在协议族使速率函数达到D(σ∥ρ)。证明了Keyl猜想：Schur采样协议在所有协变层析协议中具有最大速率函数。该最优速率函数是量子相对熵的退火版本，反映了协变层析中学习本征基的代价。

Conclusion: 协变量子层析的最优协议是Keyl的Schur采样协议，其速率函数因本征基学习成本而低于量子相对熵，表现为退火相对熵形式。同时，更一般的协议（非协变）可达到量子相对熵界。

Abstract: The problem of quantum tomography is to estimate an unknown quantum state $ρ$ from a measurement of $n$ copies of $ρ$. One can ask which tomography protocol, i.e.\ which choice of multi-copy measurement, gives the best possible estimate of $ρ$. To do so, we characterize tomography protocols by their \emph{rate function}, which governs the exponential rate at which a protocol assigns probability to a particular estimate $σ$ of the true state $ρ$. This rate function is a quantum mechanical generalization of the classical relative entropy between the true state and its estimate, and depends on the choice of protocol. It is bounded by the quantum relative entropy, and we show that this bound is sharp: for any $ρ$ and $σ$ we construct a family of protocols whose rate functions converge to the quantum relative entropy $D(σ\|ρ)$.
  We consider the family of covariant tomography protocols; these are the basis independent state estimation schemes that assume no prior information about $ρ$ and $σ$. Keyl described a specific tomography protocol based on Schur sampling, and conjectured that among all covariant tomography protocols it has the largest possible rate function for all $σ$ and $ρ$. We prove this conjecture. The resulting rate function is an annealed version of quantum relative entropy, due to the cost of learning the eigenbasis in covariant quantum state tomography.

</details>


### [141] [Diagonal-Budgeted Trotterization for Efficient Quantum Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16959)
*Srikar Chundury,Blake Burgstahler,Jiajia Li,In-Saeng Suh,Frank Mueller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出对角预算化Trotter分解策略，通过紧凑对角稀疏数据布局和专用CUDA/C++内核实现经典哈密顿量模拟，在CPU上取得最高1269倍加速，在GPU上取得最高178倍加速，同时保持近完美保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子哈密顿动力学受限于指数级状态增长和通用稀疏线性代数的高昂开销，亟需结构感知的高效方法。

Method: 提出对角预算化Trotter分解（diagonal-budgeted Trotterization），设计紧凑对角稀疏数据布局，实现专用C++/CUDA内核，利用SIMD向量化、多线程和GPU加速。

Result: 在HamLib基准测试中，CPU上TSP/MaxCut优化问题加速182-1269倍，TFIM/Heisenberg物理模型加速4.8-841倍；GPU上12-16量子比特问题加速最高178倍。

Conclusion: 对角感知的数值内核为高保真经典哈密顿量模拟提供了可扩展基础，无需指数级步数即可维持近完美保真度。

Abstract: Efficient classical simulation of quantum Hamiltonian dynamics is often bottlenecked by exponential state growth and the overhead of generic sparse linear algebra. We introduce diagonal-budgeted Trotterization, a structure-aware strategy that decomposes Hamiltonians into factors preserving diagonal sparsity while tightly controlling fidelity loss.
  Our implementation, HamSim, utilizes a compact diagonal-sparse data layout and specialized C++/CUDA kernels to bypass the overheads of generic formats like CSR. By leveraging SIMD vectorization, multithreading, and GPU acceleration, HamSim achieves high performance across heterogeneous architectures. Benchmarks on the HamLib suite show that HamSim significantly outperforms Qiskit-Aer. On CPUs, HamSim attains speedups of $182$--$1,269\times$ on optimization instances (TSP, MaxCut) and $4.8$--$841\times$ on physical models (TFIM, Heisenberg). On GPUs, it achieves up to $178\times$ speedup for $12$--$16$ qubit problems.
  Unlike traditional Trotterization, HamSim maintains near-perfect fidelity without requiring exponential steps. This demonstrates that diagonal-aware numerical kernels provide a scalable foundation for high-fidelity classical Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>


### [142] [How Many Shots Are Enough for a Quantum Circuit?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16965)
*Giuseppe Bisicchia,Alessandro Bocci,Ernesto Pimentel,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出IncrementalExecution框架，基于边际收益递减原理动态决定量子电路执行次数（shots），在黑盒设置下无需电路结构或噪声模型先验知识，实验评估涵盖33,750种配置和730万次独立实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法需要重复执行电路（shots）来估计输出分布，但确定满足目标精度的最小shots数量至关重要，以降低在当今噪声大、成本高的量子硬件上的开销和资源消耗。

Method: 提出IncrementalExecution在线框架，基于边际收益递减原理动态决定何时停止执行shots，即额外shots不再显著改变固定电路的实证分布。框架支持可定制的shots管理策略，允许在执行成本与结果保真度之间灵活权衡。

Result: 通过33,750种框架配置、180种静态电路-量子后端组合、总计730万次独立实验进行全面评估。

Conclusion: 与依赖问题特定知识或算法假设（如变分或自适应工作流）的先前工作不同，本方法适用于大量静态电路，可立即部署在当前量子云平台上。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms require repeated circuit executions, known as shots, to estimate output distributions accurately. Determining the minimal number of shots needed to meet a target accuracy is crucial to reduce costs and resource usage, especially on today's noisy and expensive quantum hardware. In this paper, we address the shot optimisation problem in a black-box setting, where no assumptions are made about the structure of the quantum circuit or the noise model of the backend. We introduce IncrementalExecution, a novel online framework that dynamically determines when to stop executing shots based on the principle of point of diminishing returns: the point at which additional shots no longer significantly alter the empirical distribution of a fixed circuit. The framework supports customisable policies for shot management, enabling flexible trade-offs between execution cost and result fidelity within static execution scenarios. We assess our proposal through an extensive experimental evaluation spanning 33,750 framework configurations across 180 unique static quantum circuit-backend combinations, for a total of 7.3M independent experiments. Unlike prior work that relies on problem-specific knowledge or algorithm-dependent assumptions (e.g., variational or adaptive workflows), our approach is applicable to a large set of static circuits and immediately deployable on current quantum cloud platforms.

</details>


### [143] [Grid-state deformation in a no-jump non-Hermitian bosonic dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17036)
*B. M. Rodriguez-Lara,H. Ghaemi-Dizicheh,S. Dehdashti,A. Hanke,A. Touhami,J. Nötzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有损耗玻色子双模系统中理想网格态的无跳跃演化，揭示了非厄米二次动力学如何通过复辛流形变形相空间中的晶格向量和原点种子，并识别了椭圆、抛物和双曲三种变形区域以及对应的振荡、线性和指数行为。在GKP态制备背景下，推导了高斯正则化的复宽度矩阵及保真条件，并展示了无跳跃动力学如何将标准的GKP+Bell态制备方案扩展为其非厄米变形，同时量化了后选择代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在差分衰减损耗下理想网格态的非厄米动力学行为，探索非厄米效应对量子信息处理资源态（如GKP态）制备和变形的影响。

Method: 采用非厄米二次哈密顿量描述有损耗玻色子双模系统，利用复辛流形分析相空间演化，通过计算晶格变形、原点种子漂移和宽度矩阵演化来刻画系统行为。

Result: 发现了椭圆（振荡）、抛物（线性）和双曲（指数）三种变形区域，存在实谱和虚谱的过渡以及例外点；全四维相空间保持行列式为1的复辛流形，但投影扇区的面积可以变化；推导了高斯正则化下宽度矩阵的演化方程及保真条件。

Conclusion: 无跳跃动力学为GKP态的非厄米变形提供了理论框架，定量给出了后选择概率代价，并揭示了非厄米系统在量子信息处理中的潜在应用。

Abstract: We study the no-jump evolution of ideal grid states in a lossy bosonic dimer with differential decay. The effective non-Hermitian quadratic dynamics induces a complex symplectic flow in phase space that deforms both the primitive lattice vectors and the origin seed. The average decay rate controls common attenuation, while coherent hopping and differential decay control the reduced dimer deformation. The reduced sector contains elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic regimes with imaginary spectra, an exceptional point, and real spectra, producing oscillatory, linear, and exponential lattice deformations. Although projected lattice areas can change, the deformation comes from a determinant-one complex symplectic flow on the full four-dimensional phase space. For a Gaussian regularization of the origin seed, we derive the associated complex width matrix and identify the positivity conditions that preserve Gaussian form. For an initial two-mode qunaught product state, the lossless limit recovers the standard beam-splitter generation of a square GKP$+$ Bell pair, while the no-jump dynamics produces its non-Hermitian deformation with a postselection cost set by the no-jump probability.

</details>

### [144] [Jahn-Teller effect in $j = 3/2$ Mott insulators: Ground states and thermal fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14851)
*Kathleen Hart,Ruairidh Sutcliffe,Arun Paramekanti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了强自旋-轨道耦合与Jahn-Teller晶格畸变在重原子量子材料中的相互作用，聚焦于简并j=3/2多重态耦合局域声子模的系统。通过SU(4)自旋蒙特卡洛方法结合JT声子，研究了Mott绝缘体中的多极基态、热相图以及温度依赖的多重态分裂，揭示了声子耦合产生的共存多极序、热JT涨落的作用以及耦合系统的熵，并讨论了在双钙钛矿和lacunar spinels等材料中的广泛意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强原子自旋-轨道耦合与Jahn-Teller晶格畸变之间的相互作用是量子材料研究的重要课题，尤其对于含有重原子的体系。简并j=3/2多重态耦合局域声子模是这类系统的原型，但其多极基态、热相图及温度依赖的光谱响应尚未得到系统阐明。

Method: 采用SU(4)自旋蒙特卡洛方法耦合JT声子，研究Mott绝缘体中j=3/2多重态的多极基态和热相图，分析温度依赖的多重态分裂。

Result: 揭示了声子耦合产生的共存多极序、热JT涨落的作用以及耦合多极-声子系统的熵。

Conclusion: 该研究对双钙钛矿Ba₂MgReO₆、Ba₂NaOsO₆以及lacunar spinels如GaTa₄Se₈和GaNb₄Se₈等材料体系具有广泛的意义。

Abstract: The interplay of strong atomic spin-orbit coupling with Jahn-Teller (JT) lattice distortions is an important theme in quantum materials hosting heavy atoms. A prototypical example of such a system is a degenerate $j = 3/2$ multiplet coupled to local phonon modes. Here, we study the multipolar ground states of this system as realized in Mott insulators, explore its thermal phase diagram via an $SU(4)$ spin Monte Carlo approach coupled to JT phonons, and study the temperature dependent splittings of the $j = 3/2$ multiplet as relevant to spectroscopic probes. Our work sheds light on coexisting distinct multipolar orders engendered by phonon coupling, role of thermal JT fluctuations, and the entropy of the coupled multipole-phonon system. We discuss broad implications for double perovskites $\rm Ba_2MgReO_6$, $\rm Ba_2NaOsO_6$ and lacunar spinels such as $\rm GaTa_4Se_8$ and $\rm GaNb_4Se_8$.

</details>


### [145] [Topological Tricritical Ising Universality Class in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15588)
*Sheng Yang,Hai-Qing Lin,Xue-Jia Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维cluster O'Brien-Fendley模型中的三临界点，发现其实现了拓扑非平凡的三临界Ising (TCI*) 普适类，具有受保护的边界二重简并和指数级的能量分裂，揭示了超越普通TCI理论的拓扑不同边界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子临界普适性如何被非平凡拓扑所丰富，特别是在一维系统中实现对称性富集的拓扑三临界Ising普适类。

Method: 研究一维cluster O'Brien-Fendley模型的三临界点，分析其边界条件、对称性富集拓扑扇区及重整化群流，比较TCI*与Ising*临界点的能量分裂标度行为。

Result: TCI*临界点具有与普通TCI相同的局域体共形数据，但实现了不同的对称性富集拓扑扇区，在开边界条件下具有受保护的二重简并；TCI*临界点的拓扑二重简并呈现指数级能量分裂，而Ising*临界点则为代数级分裂。

Conclusion: 揭示了对称性富集形式的TCI临界性，发现了超越普通TCI理论的拓扑不同边界结构，包括两种自发固定的边界条件。

Abstract: Recent advances have revealed that quantum critical universality can be enriched by nontrivial topology. Here we study the tricritical point of the one-dimensional cluster O Brien-Fendley model and show that it realizes a topologically nontrivial tricritical Ising ($\text{TCI}^*$) universality class. The transition shares the local bulk conformal data of ordinary TCI criticality, while realizing a distinct symmetry-enriched topological sector, manifested through a protected twofold degeneracy under open boundary conditions. We further show that TCI criticality admits two spontaneously fixed boundary conditions, realized respectively through symmetry enrichment and boundary renormalization-group flow, which are distinguished by the $\mathbb{Z}_2^T$ charge of the disorder field. Remarkably, we find that the topological twofold degeneracy at the $\text{TCI}^*$ critical point exhibits an exponential energy splitting, in stark contrast to the algebraic splitting at the $\text{Ising}^*$ critical point. These results reveal a symmetry-enriched form of TCI criticality and uncover topologically distinct boundary structures beyond those of the ordinary TCI theory.

</details>


### [146] [Coupled-cluster study of dynamic Jahn-Teller effect in a $5d^2$ W antifluorite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15697)
*Teruki Matsuzaki,Liviu F. Chibotaru,Maristella Alessio,Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用运动方程耦合簇（EOM-CC）理论分析了5d²双钙钛矿Cs₂WCl₆中W位点的振动耦合与磁性性质，通过模拟RIXS谱和有效磁矩，揭示了动态Jahn-Teller效应在该材料中处于弱耦合 regime，虽未引起明显结构畸变，但 modulates 谱线形状和磁矩，验证了EOM-CC方法在关联绝缘体金属位点物性预测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关联绝缘体中，电荷、自旋、轨道和晶格自由度之间的相互交织产生丰富的量子现象，但阐明这种相互作用的机制并不直接。在立方5d²双钙钛矿家族中，5d金属位点的自旋-轨道耦合基态是简并的，并与Jahn-Teller活性振动耦合，然而低温有序相中尚未有对称性降低的实验证据。本文旨在定量揭示5d²中心的本质。

Method: 应用运动方程耦合簇（EOM-CC）理论分析Cs₂WCl₆中5d² W位点的振动和磁性性质；推导电子和振动模型哈密顿量；计算W L₃边共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）谱；确定有效磁矩。

Result: 模拟的RIXS谱显示振动耦合使多个峰出现不对称；有效磁矩的温度依赖性与实验观测一致，验证了低能级分布计算的有效性；计算表明Cs₂WCl₆中Jahn-Teller效应处于弱耦合 regime，不会发生明显形变，但动态Jahn-Teller效应调制了RIXS谱的形状并影响磁矩。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了EOM-CC方法在预测关联绝缘体材料中金属位点物理现象方面的实用性。

Abstract: In correlated insulators, the interplay among coexisting charge, spin, orbital, and lattice degrees of freedom gives rise to rich quantum phenomena, while unraveling the interplay is not straightforward. In the family of cubic $5d^2$ double perovskites, the ground spin-orbit coupled electronic states of $5d$ metal sites are degenerate and couple to the Jahn-Teller active vibrations, whereas no experimental evidence of the symmetry-lowering in the low-temperature ordered phases has been reported. To quantitatively unravel the nature of $5d^2$ centers, we apply equation-of-motion coupled cluster (EOM-CC) theory to analyze the vibronic and magnetic properties of $5d^2$ W sites of Cs$_2$WCl$_6$. We derive the electronic and vibronic model Hamiltonians, calculate the W $L_3$ edge resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) spectra, and determine the effective magnetic moment. The simulated RIXS spectra show that vibronic coupling makes several peaks asymmetric. The effective magnetic moments exhibit a temperature dependence similar to that observed experimentally, confirming the validity of the calculated distribution of low-energy levels. Our calculations indicate that the Jahn-Teller effect in Cs$_2$WCl$_6$ is in a weak regime, and noticeable deformation would not occur, whereas the dynamic Jahn-Teller effect modulates the shapes of the RIXS spectra and affects the magnetic moment. This work demonstrates the usefulness of the EOM-CC method for predicting physical phenomena on metal sites in correlated insulating materials.

</details>


### [147] [Stabilizing Itinerant Electrons in a Corner-Sharing Kagomé Oxide Nd4Os3ZnO14](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15774)
*Ryutaro Okuma,Yuita Fujisawa,Chia-Hsiu Hsu,Keita Kojima,Daisuke Nishio-Hamane,Kazuki Sumida,Akio Kimura,Akira Yasui,Kohei Yamagami,Jun-ichi Yamaura,Yoshihiko Okamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过高温高压水热合成法制备的5d过渡金属kagome氧化物Nd₄Os₃ZnO₁₄。该材料具有由角共享OsO₆八面体构成的二维kagome网络，名义Os价态为+4.67。室温下呈现半金属性电子结构，冷却后转变为半导体基态并伴随空穴迁移率显著增强。磁化率测量显示局部化Nd³⁺磁矩，在2K以上无长程磁有序。金属性kagome平面、5d电子强自旋-轨道耦合与稀土磁性的共存，使其成为研究巡游体系中kagome氧化物关联电子现象的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome氧化物因其几何阻挫和特征能带拓扑结构，是探索奇异电子态的重要平台。本文旨在合成一种新的5d过渡金属kagome氧化物，以研究强自旋-轨道耦合与几何阻挫共同作用下的关联电子行为。

Method: 采用高温高压水热合成法制备Nd₄Os₃ZnO₁₄单晶；通过单晶X射线衍射确定晶体结构；利用面内电阻率和硬X射线光电子能谱表征电子结构和载流子特性；通过磁化率测量研究磁性质。

Result: 成功合成了新型kagome氧化物Nd₄Os₃ZnO₁₄；室温下呈现半金属性，低温下转变为半导体基态且空穴迁移率显著增强；观察到局部化Nd³⁺磁矩，无长程磁有序至2K。

Conclusion: Nd₄Os₃ZnO₁₄中金属性kagome平面、5d电子强自旋-轨道耦合与稀土磁性的共存，使其成为研究巡游体系中kagome氧化物关联电子现象（包括可能的新奇量子态）的理想平台。

Abstract: Kagome oxides provide a fertile platform for exploring exotic electronic states arising from geometrical frustration and characteristic band topology. Here, we report the synthesis of a 5d transition-metal kagome oxide, Nd4Os3ZnO14, obtained via high-temperature, high-pressure hydrothermal synthesis. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction reveals a two-dimensional kagome network formed by corner-sharing OsO6 octahedra, with a nominal osmium valence of +4.67. In-plane resistivity and hard X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy measurements indicate that the semimetallic electronic structure at room temperature evolves into a semiconducting ground state upon cooling, accompanied by a pronounced enhancement of hole mobility. Magnetic susceptibility measurements demonstrate localized Nd3+ moments without long-range magnetic order down to 2 K. The coexistence of a metallic kagome plane, strong spin-orbit coupling inherent to 5d electrons, and rare-earth magnetism establishes Nd4Os3ZnO14 as a promising platform for investigating correlated electron phenomena in kagome oxides within the itinerant regime.

</details>


### [148] [Probing Interfacial Magnetic Anisotropy in \texorpdfstring{CoV$_{2}$O$_{4}$}{CoV2O4} using Spin Hall Magnetoresistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15865)
*Sairam Ithineni,Krishna Jha,Aditya A. Wagh,Shwetha G. Bhat,Debashree Nayak,K. Senapati,P. S. Anil Kumar,D. Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过自旋霍尔磁电阻（SMR）测量，揭示了Pt/CVO异质结中界面磁各向异性与体磁各向异性的显著差异，并证实了双轴各向异性的存在，表明Pt/CVO是具潜力的自旋电子学平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋霍尔磁电阻（SMR）是研究复杂氧化物异质结中界面自旋输运和磁各向异性的有力工具，但Pt/CVO体系中的界面磁各向异性尚不明确，需通过角度依赖磁输运测量进行探究。

Method: 对Pt/CVO异质结进行角度依赖磁输运测量，包括面内横向SMR旋转扫描、面内纵向与横向SMR以及面外旋转下的SMR测量，并与体敏磁性测量结果进行对比。

Result: 体敏测量显示铁磁转变温度约150 K，并在90 K以下由面外各向异性转为面内各向异性；而SMR在20 K时仅在[100]方向呈现显著滞回，[110]和[1-10]方向无滞回，表明双轴各向异性且易轴沿[110]和[1-10]方向。面内SMR无突变，而面外旋转在[010]方向附近出现明显突变，表明面内各向异性存在，且该行为持续至120 K。自旋混合电导约10^14 Ω^{-1}m^{-2}。

Conclusion: Pt/CVO界面保留了与体相不同的磁各向异性，体现了SMR对界面磁性的高灵敏度；界面效应在自旋输运中起关键作用，Pt/CVO可作为有前景的自旋电子学平台。

Abstract: Spin Hall magnetoresistance (SMR) has emerged as a powerful probe for investigating interfacial spin transport and magnetic anisotropy in complex oxide heterostructures. In this work, we investigate the interfacial magnetic anisotropy in Pt/CVO through angle-dependent magnetotransport measurements. Unlike the bulk-sensitive magnetic measurements on both strained CVO and Pt/CVO films, which exhibit a ferrimagnetic transition at $T_{C} \approx 150$ K accompanied by out-of-plane anisotropy that reorients toward in-plane anisotropy below 90 K, SMR reveals a distinct interfacial magnetic anisotropy. The rotational scans of the in-plane transverse SMR at 20 K exhibit substantial hysteresis about [100], while no hysteresis is observed along [110] and [1$\bar{1}$0], indicating a biaxial anisotropy with easy axes along [110] and [1$\bar{1}$0]. Furthermore, the absence of sharp discontinuities in both the in-plane longitudinal and transverse SMR, together with pronounced discontinuities near the in-plane [010] direction during out-of-plane rotation, strongly indicates the presence of in-plane anisotropy. This behavior persists up to 120 K. The discrepancy between the bulk-sensitive magnetic measurements and the SMR response suggests that the Pt/CVO interface retains a magnetic anisotropy distinct from the bulk, highlighting the interfacial sensitivity of SMR. Additionally, the spin mixing conductance is found to be of the order of $10^{14}$ $Ω^{-1}\mathrm{m}^{-2}$, comparable to other oxide-based spintronic systems. These findings highlight the crucial role of interfacial effects in spin transport and establish Pt/CVO as a promising platform for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [149] [Staggered Virtual-Loop-Current Order in Pseudospin-1 Dirac Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16155)
*Yi-Chun Hung,Arun Bansil*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了在骰子晶格的部分填充赝自旋-1狄拉克平带中，短程相互作用可以驱动交错虚环流（SVLC）序和√3×√3电荷序。该SVLC序源于电荷密度的量子涨落，在邻近三角形格子间形成交替循环，导致时间反演对称性破缺，并产生有限的内禀反常霍尔电导和轨道磁化。研究表明，赝自旋-1狄拉克锥通过其平带波函数的等价性进入SVLC序，不同于赝自旋-1/2系统依赖低能谷结构。这项工作建立了更高赝自旋狄拉克系统作为研究平带物理中奇异涌现序的新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索赝自旋-1/2平带狄拉克材料中相互作用驱动的奇异相已被广泛研究，但更高赝自旋系统能否实现全新类型的序以及这些序如何受底层狄拉克态性质支配仍是一个未解决问题。

Method: 通过理论分析和受限实空间Hartree-Fock计算，研究骰子晶格部分填充赝自旋-1狄拉克平带中短程相互作用驱动的交错虚环流序。

Result: 发现短程相互作用可驱动SVLC序和√3×√3电荷序；SVLC序引起时间反演对称性破缺，诱导有限反常霍尔电导和轨道磁化；SVLC是弱相互作用下能量最低的解。

Conclusion: 更高赝自旋狄拉克系统可作为研究平带物理中奇异涌现序的新平台。

Abstract: Although interactions are known to generate exotic phases in pseudospin-1/2 flat-band Dirac materials, it remains an open question whether the higher-pseudospin systems could realize entirely new types of orders and, if so, how such orders are governed by the nature of the underlying Dirac states. Here, we demonstrate that short-range interactions can drive a staggered virtual-loop-current (SVLC) order together with a $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ charge order in a partially filled pseudospin-1 Dirac flat band of the dice lattice. The virtual loop currents are shown to originate from interaction-driven quantum fluctuations of charge densities and exhibit alternating circulation between the neighboring triangular plaquettes. The resulting spontaneous time-reversal symmetry breaking is found to induce finite intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity and orbital magnetization. The SVLC state is shown to be the lowest energy solution in restricted real-space Hartree-Fock calculations in the weakly interacting regime. The pseudospin-1 Dirac cones enter the SVLC order through the equivalence of their flat-band wavefunctions, and unlike the pseudospin-1/2 Dirac systems, do not rely on the presence of well-defined low-energy valleys in the electronic spectrum. Our study establishes higher-pseudospin Dirac systems as a new platform for investigating exotic emergent orders in flat-band physics.

</details>


### [150] [Evaluating the Structural Basis for Polar Altermagnet Candidate Ca$_{3}$(Ru,Ti)$_{2}$O$_{7}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16181)
*Akash Saha,Yihuang Xiong,Vladimir A. Stoica,Subin Mali,Aaron Pearre,Saugata Sarker,Huaiyu Wang,Yufei Zhao,Evguenia Karapetrova,Yu Wang,Jadupati Nag,Zachary W. Hazenstab,Seng Huat Lee,Long-Qing Chen,Geoffroy Hautier,Binghai Yan,Zhiqiang Mao,Venkatraman Gopalan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度X射线衍射实验发现，Ca₃Ru₂O₇中并未检测到DFT预测的Pn2₁a极性交变磁结构（原子位移<60-200 fm，远小于DFT预测的~1 pm），表明其电子相变不伴随晶格对称性变化，而Ti掺杂超过~3%时才会出现Bb2₁m交变磁相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极性序与交变磁序之间的相互作用，验证DFT预测的Ca₃Ru₂O₇中Pn2₁a极性交变磁相是否存在。

Method: 使用同步辐射X射线衍射对Ca₃Ru₂O₇和Ca₃(Ru₀.₉₉Ti₀.₀₁)₂O₇单晶进行亚皮米级精度的结构搜索。

Result: 未检测到Pn2₁a相（原子位移灵敏度60-200 fm，远低于DFT预测的~1 pm）；Ti掺杂超过~3%时出现Bb2₁m交变磁相。

Conclusion: Ca₃Ru₂O₇是一个强电子关联驱动电子相变而不伴随晶格对称性变化的独特体系；亚皮米级计量学对区分交变磁体的结构起源与电子起源至关重要。

Abstract: The interplay between polar and altermagnetic orders remains largely unexplored in the broader landscape of correlated electron systems. Ca$_{3}$Ru$_{2}$O$_{7}$ has been proposed by density functional theory (DFT) as a polar altermagnet, reliant on the transformation of experimentally reported $Bb2_{1}m$ phase to a lower symmetry $Pn2_{1}a$ structure. Here, we perform a targeted search for the $Pn2_{1}a$ phase using synchrotron X-ray diffraction on single crystals of Ca$_{3}$Ru$_{2}$O$_{7}$ and Ca$_{3}$(Ru$_{0.99}$Ti$_{0.01}$)$_{2}$O$_{7}$. No diffraction signature of the $Pn2_{1}a$ structure is detected down to 20 K within experimental limits of $\sim$60-200 fm atomic displacements, significantly smaller than the DFT prediction of $\sim$1 pm. Combined with recent nonlinear transport measurements, our structural study suggests Ca$_{3}$Ru$_{2}$O$_{7}$ as a unique system where strong electron correlations drive an electronic phase transition without any measurable lattice symmetry change. With Ti substitution exceeding $\sim$3%, a chemically tunable altermagnetic phase with $Bb2_{1}m$ structure emerges. The study highlights the importance of sub-picometer metrology towards de-convolving structural versus electronic origins of altermagnets.

</details>


### [151] [symveig: Verified eigenvalue enclosures for symmetry-decomposed Hermitian matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16217)
*Sarang Vehale,Ritu Goel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文介绍 symveig，一个纯 NumPy/SciPy 包，用于对量子晶格哈密顿量进行带严格误差验证的特征值包络计算，通过对称性分解在精度和速度上均显著优于全矩阵验证，所有包络半宽均为 IEEE 754 算术下特征值误差的保证上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子晶格哈密顿量的精确对角化返回未认证精度的浮点特征值，舍入误差、求解器行为和病态性可能在无警告情况下破坏结果，因此需要一种可机检的严格特征值包络方法。

Method: 基于纯 NumPy/SciPy 实现，对与阿贝尔守恒量（如总磁化强度）对易的哈密顿矩阵，按对称性扇区独立验证；每个扇区块远小于全矩阵，无需形成或对角化全矩阵；通过显式浮点误差分析（无启发式松弛）在 IEEE 754 舍入到最近算术下获得保证上界。

Result: 在系统尺寸 L=4-12 时，扇区分解使包络更紧（紧 3-9 倍），速度更快（L=12 时加速比达 130 倍）；在 1D Heisenberg（开边界和周期边界）、J1-J2 Heisenberg 和 2D Heisenberg 晶格上验证，所有测试配置中每个计算特征值均被其包络包含。

Conclusion: symveig 将严格验证的特征值包络引入标准科学 Python 栈，无需 INTLAB 或 MATLAB，为量子多体物理中的可靠数值计算提供了可认证的工具。

Abstract: Exact diagonalization of quantum lattice Hamiltonians returns floating-point eigenvalues whose accuracy is not certified: rounding error, eigensolver behaviour, and ill-conditioning can corrupt a result without warning. We present \texttt{symveig}, a pure NumPy/SciPy package that computes rigorous, machine-checkable enclosures of all eigenvalues of a Hermitian matrix, with an optional symmetry-sector decomposition. For a matrix that commutes with an abelian conserved quantity diagonal in the working basis (for example the total magnetization of a spin model), the package verifies each symmetry sector independently. Because each sector block is much smaller than the full matrix, this yields enclosures that are both tighter (by a factor of $3$-$9$ across system sizes $L = 4$-$12$) and dramatically faster (a wall-clock speedup of up to $130\times$ at $L = 12$) than verifying the full matrix, while never forming or diagonalizing it. Every enclosure half-width is a guaranteed upper bound on the distance from a computed eigenvalue to the nearest true eigenvalue under IEEE~754 round-to-nearest arithmetic, obtained by explicit floating-point error analysis with no heuristic slack. The implementation requires neither INTLAB nor MATLAB, bringing rigorously verified eigenvalue enclosure into the standard scientific-Python stack used in computational physics. We validate the package on $1$D Heisenberg (open and periodic), $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg, and $2$D Heisenberg lattices, confirming that every computed eigenvalue is contained in its enclosure across all tested configurations.

</details>


### [152] [Interfacial control of magnetism and electron transport in nonisostructural SrRuO$_3$/SrCuO$_2$ heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16235)
*Digbijaya Palai,B. Maharana,P. Biswal,Shwetha G. Bhat,D. Nayak,D. Sahoo,R. Soni,K. Senapati,Z. Hossain,D. Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建结构不对称的SrRuO₃/SrCuO₂异质结，发现界面显著增强了饱和磁化强度、垂直磁各向异性和反常霍尔效应的本征贡献，同时抑制了低温柔乱诱导的量子输运修正，实现了磁性、各向异性与电子输运的多重协同调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用结构不对称氧化物异质界面处的晶格与电子自由度失配，探索实现新的功能特性并调控物理性质，为氧化物电子学提供潜在应用。

Method: 对比研究4 nm SrRuO₃单层膜与SrRuO₃/SrCuO₂双层异质结的磁性和电子输运性质，包括磁化强度、垂直磁各向异性、低温电阻率温度依赖关系以及反常霍尔效应的标度分析。

Result: SRO/SCO双层膜饱和磁化强度(～2.7 μB/Ru)和有效各向异性常数(～2.13×10⁶ erg/cc)较SRO单层膜(1.3 μB/Ru, 5.25×10⁵ erg/cc)显著提升；低温5 K以下电阻率回升被抑制，呈现费米液体输运(ρ∝T²)至2 K；反常霍尔效应显示本征贝里曲率主导机制，且本征和斜散射贡献在双层中增强。

Conclusion: 界面可作为有效调控手段，同时增强磁化强度、垂直磁各向异性和反常霍尔效应的本征散射贡献，并抑制无序引起的量子输运修正，表明界面在修饰费米能级附近电子能带结构中起关键作用。

Abstract: Heterointerfaces between structurally dissimilar oxides provide a platform to exploit the interfacial mismatch in the lattice and electronic degrees of freedom to realize emergent functionalities and tunable physical properties with potential applications in oxide electronics. Here, we investigate the magnetic and electronic transport properties of symmetry-mismatched SrRuO$_3$ (SRO)/SrCuO$_2$ (SCO) heterostructure, in which a 4-nm-thick itinerant ferromagnetic metal SRO is interfaced with a planar-type antiferromagnetic insulator SCO, in comparison with a reference SRO (4 nm) film. While both the bare SRO and SRO/SCO bilayer exhibit perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA), the SRO/SCO bilayer shows a pronounced enhancement in the saturation magnetization (M$_S$ $\approx$ 2.7 $μ_B$/Ru) and effective anisotropy constant (K$_{\mathrm{eff}}$ $\approx$ 2.13 $\times$ 10$^6$ erg/cc) relative to the bare SRO film (M$_S$ $\approx$ 1.3 $μ_B$/Ru and K$_{\mathrm{eff}}$ $\approx$ 5.25 $\times$ 10$^5$ erg/cc). Interestingly, the low-temperature resistivity upturn below 5 K arising from disorder-induced quantum corrections in bare SRO is strongly suppressed in SRO/SCO, and it exhibits Fermi-liquid-like transport ($ρ\propto T^2$) down to 2 K. Analysis of the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) reveals dominant intrinsic Berry-curvature driven electron transport in both samples, with enhanced intrinsic and skew-scattering contributions in the SRO/SCO bilayer, indicating the critical role of the interface in modifying the electronic band structure near the Fermi level. Our results demonstrate that the interface acts as an effective control knob, concurrently enhancing the magnetization, PMA, and intrinsic scattering contribution to the AHE in the SRO/SCO heterostructure, while suppressing the disorder-driven quantum corrections to transport behavior observed in the bare SRO film.

</details>


### [153] [Compact Spin-Charge Separated Neural Quantum States for Valence-Bond States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17045)
*Ang-Kun Wu,Louis Primeau,Yixin Zhang,Jingtao Zhang,Adrian Del Maestro,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解点引导的神经网络量子态（NQS）架构设计策略，通过物理启发的四个设计（步幅匹配的局部规则卷积、几何池化、符号解析激活函数、显式自旋-空穴分离）在准一维VBS及掺杂sVBS态上实现了高保真度，参数效率显著优于全连接、卷积和Transformer基线，并展示了局部规则的可迁移性和L²参数缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NQS的效率高度依赖于架构是否反映目标态的符号结构和约束希尔伯特空间，现有架构对此缺乏系统性设计原则，需要一种物理启发的架构设计方法。

Method: 提出可解点引导策略：先在精确可解点设计反映正确局部规则的架构，再通过增大核大小和隐藏维度微调至非精确区域。具体包含四个物理启发设计：步幅匹配的局部规则卷积、几何池化、tanh(x^{2k+1})符号解析激活函数、显式自旋-空穴扇区分离。

Result: 在准一维VBS和掺杂sVBS态上，该架构以更少参数达到高保真度，优于全连接、卷积和Transformer基线。自旋扇区的局部规则可从小系统迁移至大系统。远离可解点时，增大核大小和隐藏维度可系统提升精度，参数缩放约L²，而MPS在无间隙区约为L⁴。

Conclusion: 该工作为符号结构化和约束希尔伯特空间中的紧凑NQS提供了设计蓝图，并为进一步发展t-J和Hubbard模型族的物理启发架构铺平了道路。

Abstract: Neural-network quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible nonlinear representation of quantum many-body wavefunctions, but their efficiency depends sensitively on whether the architecture reflects the sign structure and constrained Hilbert space of the target state. In this work, we propose a solvable-point-guided strategy: design the architecture at an exactly solvable point where the correct local rules can be read off, then refine to the non-exact regime by enlarging only the kernel size and hidden dimension. The strategy is built from four physics-motivated designs: a stride-matched local-rule convolution, geometric pooling, a sign-resolving $\tanh(x^{2k+1})$ activation, and explicit spin-hole sector separation. We test this approach on quasi-one-dimensional valence-bond-solid (VBS) states and their doped soliton variants (sVBS), the exact ground states of a $t$-$J$-like model with a single mobile hole. In finite-size benchmarks, this architecture reaches high fidelity for the exact sVBS state with substantially fewer parameters than generic fully connected, convolutional, and transformer baselines tested under the same setup. For the spin sector, the learned local rule transfers from small to larger systems without retraining. Away from the solvable point, increasing kernel size and hidden dimension systematically improves accuracy, and the model shows approximately $L^2$ parameter scaling in the gapless regime for system size $L$, compared with approximately $L^4$ for matrix-product states in the same regime. Our work establishes a recipe for compact NQS in sign-structured, constrained Hilbert spaces and paves the pathway to physics-informed architectures for the broader $t$-$J$ and Hubbard families.

</details>


### [154] [Orbital-selective band evolution and out-of-plane correlation in the FeGe-family kagome antiferromagnet ScFe$_6$Ge$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16245)
*Jae Hyuck Lee,Ze Yan,Tongrui Li,Yichen Yang,Dirk Wulferding,Jongkeun Jung,Zhicheng Jiang,Mao Ye,Zhengtai Liu,Changyoung Kim,Soohyun Cho,Yanfeng Guo,Dawei Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对比研究ScFe₆Ge₆（无CDW序）与FeGe（有CDW序），发现ScFe₆Ge₆在195K发生磁转变，伴随轨道选择性行为、电子-声子耦合和磁弹耦合，表明轨道选择物理学和面外关联在磁性kagome金属中实现自旋关联CDW序中起增强作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联材料（如高温超导体）中电荷密度波（CDW）序与磁性之间的关系尚不清楚。FeGe是首个在反铁磁态中表现出CDW序的kagome金属，但其自旋关联CDW序的普适条件有待阐明。

Method: 选取无CDW序的ScFe₆Ge₆作为对比体系（保留FeGe的其他性质但降低电荷自由度参与），结合角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）和拉曼光谱，研究其磁转变（T*=195K）前后的电子结构和晶格动力学行为。

Result: ScFe₆Ge₆在195K发生磁转变而非CDW转变；该转变表现出仅局限于单个kagome Dirac带的轨道选择性行为，以及电子-声子耦合和磁弹耦合（与面外声子模相关）。

Conclusion: 轨道选择性物理和面外关联在实现磁性kagome金属中自旋关联CDW序的过程中起着增强作用。

Abstract: In strongly correlated materials such as high-temperature superconductors, the relation between charge density wave (CDW) order and magnetism remains an important unresolved problem. FeGe is the first kagome metal known to exhibit CDW order deep within an antiferromagnetic state, accompanied by an unconventional evolution of lattice symmetry. To elucidate the general conditions governing such spin-correlated CDW order, we investigate ScFe$_6$Ge$_6$, which lacks CDW order and therefore exhibits reduced involvement of charge degrees of freedom while retaining other properties of FeGe. Instead of a CDW order, ScFe$_6$Ge$_6$ undergoes a magnetic transition at $T^*$ = 195 K. Across this transition, angle-resolved photoemission and Raman spectroscopy reveal orbital-selective behavior confined to a single kagome Dirac band, together with electron-phonon and magnetoelastic coupling to an out-of-plane phonon mode. These results suggest that orbital-selective physics and out-of-plane correlations play enhanced roles in realizing spin-correlated CDW order in magnetic kagome metals.

</details>


### [155] [Multiband transport hierarchy and large Nernst effect in EuAuBi: Establishing a Nernst scaling for asymmetric multiband systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16249)
*Weimin Quan,Xiaodong Guo,Lingxiao Zhao,Shengwei Chi,Gang Xu,Zengwei Zhu,Xiaokang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在极性半金属EuAuBi中，稀密电子与空穴口袋共存。研究发现：空穴主导纵向电阻率，霍尔效应随场强从电子主导转为空穴主导，塞贝克系数低温由电子控制、高温由双口袋控制。Nernst效应完全由超高迁移率电子口袋主导，产生约5 μV/K的大信号，且遵循标度律ν/T ∝ μ_e/E_{F,tot}，表明这属于半经典多带现象而非拓扑贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关联材料中，共存的不同载流子密度的口袋引发两个基本问题：哪个口袋主导各类输运系数？传统单带Nernst标度律ν/T ∝ μ/E_F在多带系统中是否仍然成立？本文旨在回答这两个问题。

Method: 以极性半金属EuAuBi为研究对象（稀电子口袋n_e~10^16 cm^{-3}，密空穴口袋n_h~10^21 cm^{-3}），通过测量纵向电阻率、霍尔效应、塞贝克系数和Nernst效应，结合双带热电导率分析，揭示各输运系数的口袋主导机制，并推导多带系统中的Nernst标度律。

Result: 空穴口袋主导纵向电阻率；霍尔效应随场强从电子主导转为空穴主导；塞贝克系数低温由电子口袋主导，高温由双口袋共同主导；Nernst效应完全由超高迁移率电子口袋主导，在202 K、~1 T下产生~5 μV/K的大信号；Nernst系数遵循标度律ν/T ∝ μ_e/E_{F,tot}，该标度源于电子-空穴电导率比与费米能之比间的补偿机制。

Conclusion: EuAuBi中大Nernst效应是半经典多带现象而非拓扑贝利曲率贡献，这一认识推进了多带电子系统的热电输运物理，并为低场横向热电设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: In correlated materials, coexisting pockets of vastly different carrier densities raise two fundamental questions: which pocket governs the various transport coefficients, and does the conventional Nernst scaling $ν/T \propto μ/E_F$, originally derived for single-band systems, still hold? We address both questions in the polar semimetal EuAuBi, where a dilute electron pocket ($n_e \sim 10^{16}~\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$) coexists with a dense hole pocket ($n_h \sim 10^{21}~\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$). We find a clear hierarchy: the hole pocket dominates the longitudinal resistivity; the Hall effect crosses from electron- to hole-dominance with increasing field; the Seebeck coefficient is dominated by the electron pocket at low temperature and by both pockets at high temperature. Remarkably, the Nernst effect is governed entirely by the ultrahigh-mobility electron pocket, yielding a large low-field signal of $\sim 5~μ\mathrm{V/K}$ near 1~T at 202~K, comparable to anomalous Nernst signals in magnetic Weyl semimetals. By analyzing the two-band thermoelectric conductivity, we show that the Nernst coefficient follows a scaling $ν/T \propto μ_e/{E_{F, tot}}$. This scaling originates from a compensation between the electron-to-hole conductivity ratio and the Fermi-energy ratio, establishing that the large Nernst effect is a semiclassical multiband phenomenon rather than a topological Berry-curvature contribution. This understanding advances the thermoelectric transport physics of multiband electronic systems and offers a guiding principle for low-field transverse thermoelectric design.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum oscillations in proximity to high-angular-momentum band inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16397)
*Jiahao Yang,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究高角动量能带反转模型中的量子振荡现象，发现手征双带模型在陈绝缘体中存在热激活振荡，而时间反演对称四带模型中两个时间反演块之间的干涉导致偏离标准Lifshitz-Kosevich公式，产生非单调温度依赖和特征抑制温度，其数量由角动量l决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子振荡是探测磁场中电子结构的基本手段，可揭示金属费米面拓扑和绝缘体中的准粒子性质。本文旨在研究高角动量（HAM）能带反转这一特殊拓扑结构中的量子振荡行为，探索其区别于常规系统的特征。

Method: 分别构建手征双带模型和时间反演不变四带模型，通过理论分析研究高角动量能带反转情况下的量子振荡现象，计算振荡幅度对温度的依赖关系。

Result: 1) 手征双带模型中，杂化陈绝缘体区域因热激活激发出现有限振荡；2) 四带模型中，两个时间反演块的干涉导致振荡幅度呈非单调温度依赖，偏离Lifshitz-Kosevich公式，并出现特征抑制温度，其数量等于角动量l。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别了高角动量能带反转在实验上可观测的特征信号，并为其他离散对称性相关的杂化和激子配对现象提供了分析框架。

Abstract: Quantum oscillations provide a fundamental probe of electronic structure in magnetic fields, revealing the Fermi surface topology in metals, and/or the quasiparticle properties even in the insulating regimes. Here we study quantum oscillations in minimal models of high-angular-momentum (HAM) band inversion for both a chiral two-band model and a time-reversal-invariant four-band model. In the former case, finite oscillations can appear at the hybridized Chern-insulating regime due to thermal-activated excitations. In the latter case, interference between the two time-reversal-related blocks drives a strong deviation from the Lifshitz-Kosevich form, producing a non-monotonic temperature dependence of the oscillation amplitude and characteristic suppression temperatures whose number follows the angular momentum $l$. These results identify experimentally accessible signatures of HAM band inversion and provide a framework for other discrete-symmetry-related hybridizations and excitonic pairings.

</details>


### [157] [Intervalley coherence and flavor polarization in three-valley moiré systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16622)
*Jeyong Park,Laura Classen,Mathias S. Scheurer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭曲M点材料莫尔超晶格中由相互作用诱导的对称性破缺，识别并分类了包括谷间相干（IVC）序在内的多种不稳定性，并通过强耦合框架和哈特里-福克计算验证了这些序的出现机制，展示了这类材料为关联物理提供的丰富平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扭曲M点材料（三个谷）中相互作用诱导的对称性破缺，分类可能的候选不稳定性，特别是IVC序，并与已知的两谷体系（如石墨烯）进行对比。

Method: 基于一般对称性论证进行分类，采用解析强耦合框架与无限制哈特里-福克（Hartree-Fock）方法对完整连续模型进行计算，两种方法结果高度一致。

Result: IVC序在中等带宽下因超交换机制出现，在强耦合区域也会因偏离平带条件而产生；IVC态可以等同耦合所有三个谷，也可以具有非平庸符号结构或不同耦合强度。

Conclusion: 扭曲M点材料提供了丰富的关联物理研究平台，与扭曲多层石墨烯既有差异也有相似之处。

Abstract: We investigate interaction-induced symmetry breaking in moiré superlattices created by twisting two identical materials where the electronic low-energy degrees of freedom reside in the vicinity of the $M$ points. Based on general symmetry arguments, we identify and classify the possible candidate instabilities that, besides flavor polarized states, also involve a variety of intervalley-coherent (IVC) orders. This complexity is related primarily to the presence of three valleys, instead of the well-studied scenario of two, e.g., in graphene: IVC states can couple all three valleys identically, with a non-trivial sign structure, or even with different magnitudes. We study the energetics using an analytical strong-coupling framework and unrestricted Hartree-Fock applied to the full continuum model, with very good agreement between the two approaches. Interestingly, depending on stacking, IVC instabilities not only appear due to superexchange at moderate bandwidths, but also deep in the strong-coupling regime as a result of deviations from the flat-metric condition. Our work demonstrates that twisted $M$-point materials provide a rich playground for complex correlated physics and highlights differences and similarities to twisted multilayer graphene.

</details>


### [158] [Field-selected seven-site topological magnons in a classical frustrated triangular-lattice K-$Γ$-$Γ'$ magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16704)
*Bin Xi,Jie Lu,Shun-Li Yu,Yafang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过经典自旋基态搜索和线性自旋波理论，研究了三角晶格K-Gamma-Gamma'模型在强阻挫磁体中的磁子拓扑性质，发现面内磁场选择的非共面七位序可作为可控经典参考态，其磁子能带具有场可调陈数，并导致拓扑相变和热霍尔电导率符号变化，为阻挫磁体中的磁子拓扑提供了半经典基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强阻挫磁体中磁子拓扑的定义常因零场下缺乏简谐磁子真空而受阻，本文旨在通过场选态提供可控经典参考系，从而研究磁子拓扑。

Method: 采用经典自旋基态搜索结合线性自旋波理论（LSWT），分析三角晶格K-Gamma-Gamma'模型在占优Gamma交换区间的面内磁场响应。

Result: 发现磁场选取的紧凑非共面七位序；磁子能带陈数场可调，且随磁场增大发生狄拉克型能带接触，使Berry曲率在第五和第六能带间转移，陈向量从(0,1,0,-2,1,0,0)变为(0,1,0,-2,-1,2,0)；该拓扑转变重组了带分辨热霍尔电导率，使总κ_{xy}(T)在较高能带被热占据时出现近零穿越；约一半相干谱权重位于陈活性支路上。

Conclusion: 该结果为纯最近邻交换模型中的磁子拓扑提供了可控的半经典基准，并指出通过动力学结构因子可光谱识别拓扑支路。

Abstract: Defining magnon topology in strongly frustrated magnets is often hindered by the absence of a simple harmonic magnon vacuum at zero field. Within a classical-spin ground-state search followed by linear spin-wave theory (LSWT), we demonstrate that a representative triangular-lattice K-Gamma-Gamma' model in a dominant-Gamma exchange regime has an in-plane-field-selected compact, noncoplanar seven-site order. This field-selected state provides a controlled classical reference state and hosts magnon bands with field-tunable Chern numbers. Increasing the field drives a Dirac-like band touching that transfers Berry curvature between the fifth and sixth bands, altering the Chern vector from (0, 1, 0, -2, 1, 0, 0) to (0, 1, 0, -2, -1, 2, 0). This topological transition reorganizes the band-resolved thermal Hall conductivity, driving the total $κ_{xy}(T)$ through a near-zero crossing once the upper bands are thermally populated. The dynamical structure factor places roughly half of the coherent spectral weight on the Chern-active branches, offering a spectroscopic route to identify the topological branches. These results define a controlled semiclassical benchmark for magnon topology in this pure nearest-neighbor exchange model.

</details>


### [159] [Spin-orbit-entangled $J_{\rm eff}=\frac{1}{2}$ magnetism and unconventional spin freezing in the bond-disordered pyrochlore antiferromagnet NaCdCo$_2$F$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16875)
*Andrej Kancko,Hironori Sakai,Javier Herrero-Martín,Adam Berlie,Marc Uhlarz,Tetiana Haidamak,Yo Tokunaga,Ross H. Colman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了键无序烧绿石反铁磁体NaCdCo₂F₇，发现其由于自旋-轨道耦合的Co²⁺离子（Jeff=1/2，具有显著的未淬灭轨道贡献）实现了一种非常规的、具有持续动力学的自旋玻璃态，与同构的S=1/2 NaCdCu₂F₇实现的随机单态截然不同，表明自旋-轨道耦合是决定键无序阻挫烧绿石磁体基态的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究键无序阻挫烧绿石反铁磁体中，局域磁矩的性质（特别是自旋-轨道耦合的作用）如何从根本上影响量子基态。

Method: 通过高场磁化测量、Co L₂₃边XAS/XMCD表征电子态，结合μSR和²³Na NMR谱学手段探测自旋动力学行为，并与同构的NaCdCu₂F₇进行对比。

Result: 1) 确认Co²⁺离子为自旋-轨道纠缠的Jeff=1/2态，具有显著的未淬灭轨道贡献，表现局域XY各向异性；2) 在~10 K以下自旋波动逐渐减慢，形成部分冻结态并保持低温持续动力学，偏离典型自旋玻璃行为；3) 与随机单态NaCdCu₂F₇相比，二者对键无序的响应完全不同。

Conclusion: 自旋-轨道耦合是控制键无序阻挫烧绿石磁体基态命运的关键因素。

Abstract: Bond disorder in frustrated pyrochlore antiferromagnets can give rise to fundamentally different quantum ground states depending on the nature of the local magnetic moments. Here, we show that the bond-disordered pyrochlore NaCdCo$_2$F$_7$ realizes an unconventional spin-glass-like state with continued dynamics, in stark contrast to its isostructural $S=\frac{1}{2}$ NaCdCu$_2$F$_7$ counterpart. High-field magnetization and Co $L_{2,3}$-edge XAS/XMCD establish spin-orbit-entangled $J_{\rm eff}=\frac{1}{2}$ Co$^{2+}$ moments with a substantial unquenched orbital contribution, consistent with local $XY$ anisotropy seen in the isostructural Na$A''$Co$_2$F$_7$ ($A''$ = Ca, Sr) analogues. $μ$SR and $^{23}$Na NMR measurements reveal progressive slowing of spin fluctuations below $\sim10$ K, culminating in a partially frozen state with persistent low-temperature dynamics that deviates from a canonical spin glass. Comparison with the isostructural bond-disordered pyrochlore NaCdCu$_2$F$_7$, which realizes a random-singlet state, reveals a fundamentally different response of spin-orbit-entangled Co$^{2+}$ moments to bond disorder. These results identify spin-orbit coupling as a key ingredient governing the fate of bond-disordered frustrated pyrochlore magnets.

</details>


### [160] [Electronic Green's function across the pseudogap to stripe transition in the $t$-$t'$-$J$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16968)
*Martin Ulaga,Aritra Sinha,Alexander Wietek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用张量网络算法研究欠掺杂t-t'-J模型，发现赝隙的形成与t'参数密切相关，并随温度变化表现出从相干条纹序到涨落电荷簇的转变，节点间隙闭合填满，形成费米弧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联电子体系中超导圆顶伴随的电荷密度波与赝隙之间的内在联系，特别是赝隙的出现机制及其与电荷密度波的关系尚不明确。

Method: 采用张量网络算法在宽度为4的圆柱上计算有限温度动力学下的欠掺杂t-t'-J模型的电子格林函数。

Result: 发现赝隙的标志——动量分化强烈依赖于t'参数；随着温度降低，动量依赖的能隙打开，与中间温度下费米弧的形成一致；节点（π/2,π/2）附近的能隙随着温度升高而闭合填满，伴随着相干条纹序熔化为涨落电荷簇。

Conclusion: 赝隙的形成与t'密切相关，且与条纹序和电荷簇涨落之间存在动态关联，为理解铜氧化物超导体中赝隙与电荷密度波的关系提供了微观机制。

Abstract: Superconducting domes in strongly correlated electronic systems are often accompanied by charge density waves and peculiar features in the electronic structure. The appearance of a pseudogap, in particular, and its relation to charge density waves remains insufficiently understood. Here, we investigate the electronic Green's function of the underdoped $t$-$t'$-$J$ model of the cuprate superconductors using tensor network algorithms for finite temperature dynamics on cylinders of width $4$. We find the prominent momentum differentiation, the hallmark of the pseudogap, to be strongly dependent on $t'$, which develops into a momentum-dependent opening of a gap upon decreasing temperature, consistent with the formation of a Fermi arc at intermediate temperatures. The nodal gap around ${\bf k}=(π/2,π/2)$ closes and fills with increasing temperature as coherent stripe order melts into a regime of fluctuating charge clusters.

</details>


### [161] [Disentangling multi-spin dynamic correlations in the Heisenberg spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16992)
*V. K . Bhartiya,U. Kumar,T. Kim,S. Fan,S. Okamoto,M. Mitrano,M. P. M. Dean,J. Pelliciari,I. A. Zaliznyak,S. Johnston,V. Bisogni*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了通过共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）的能量失谐技术，可以选择性地探测海森堡自旋-1/2链中的双自旋和多自旋动力学关联，从而为研究量子材料中的多体动力学提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶关联对于理解奇特相和揭示量子动力学的普适性至关重要。然而，非弹性中子散射只能测量双粒子关联，而涉及两个以上粒子的关联测量在固体中仍是一项重大挑战。

Method: 以海森堡自旋-1/2链中的磁激发为研究对象，利用RIXS通过能量失谐激发不同的中间态，结合理论建模分离双自旋和多自旋响应，并通过t-J哈密顿量的精确对角化进行验证。

Result: (i) 双自旋与多自旋动力学关联的共振能量间隔约为3J/4，表明多自旋部分的散射截面来自包含自旋翻转的中间态；(ii) 双自旋通道的谱权重呈现洛伦兹型共振能量分布，而多自旋通道呈现高斯型分布，表明后者涉及多个中间自旋构型。洛伦兹和高斯共振能量分布的宽度分别主要由核心空穴寿命(Γ/2)和2J/Γ决定。

Conclusion: 通过RIXS能量失谐作为调控手段，可以实现对多自旋动力学的受控访问，为探索量子材料中的多体动力学开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Higher-order correlations are essential for understanding exotic phases and uncovering universal aspects of quantum dynamics. While inelastic neutron scattering provides well-established access to two-particle correlations, measuring correlations in solids involving more than two particles remains a major challenge. Focusing on magnetic excitations in the Heisenberg spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ chain, we demonstrate that resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) can selectively probe multi-spin dynamical correlations by exciting distinct intermediate states through energy detuning. Through theoretical modeling, we isolate the two- and multi- spin responses, and establish that: (i) The resonant energies of the two- and multi- spin dynamical correlations are separated by $\approx \frac{3}{4}J$; implying that the multi-spin part of the cross-section comes from intermediate states that contain spin flips; (ii) The spectral weight of the two-spin channel exhibits a Lorentzian resonant energy profile consistent with a single dominant electronic configuration, whereas the multi-spin channel response shows a Gaussian-like profile, implying contributions from multiple intermediate spin configurations. These characteristics are reproduced by exact diagonalization of the $t-J$ Hamiltonian, which further reveals that the widths of Lorentzian and Gaussian resonant energy profiles depend primarily on core-hole lifetime ($Γ/2$) and $\frac{2J}Γ$, respectively. Controlled access to multi-spin dynamics, using RIXS energy detuning as a knob, can open new pathways to explore many-body dynamics in quantum materials.

</details>
